- **Account-only, said plainly**: a guest deck's dialog (this browser holds its key) reads "Sign in to keep a game journal" linked to `/account?next=/decks/<id>/edit` with "Your games are kept on your account, with the exact list you played." and asks nothing; a 401 (a signed-out owner) reads the same. Games… shows on every live deck whose game declares a journal (both do); drafts have none — the draft More pin is unchanged.
- **Segmented** gained `pointer-coarse:min-w-11` on its items: the dev pass found Players' one-digit segments 24 px wide on a phone; every other call site's labels were already wider (a no-op there), and R6's "44 px on coarse pointers" now holds for width too.
- **Found before it shipped**: drizzle prints a select list's columns unqualified, so a correlated `"deck_id" = "id"` in GET's counts would have compared `deck_games.deck_id` to `deck_games.id` — the counts bind the deck id instead (route-test pinned).
- **Lifecycle**: `deck_games` joins `USER_TABLES`; the privacy page says what a logged game stores and that deleting a deck or the account takes its versions and games (the account page's danger zone says it too); the comments in `delete-account.ts` and `forks.ts` (forks copy no games); `smoke:account` checks "her games gone" (and the played snapshot with them); `smoke:games` is new; the restore drill checks the identity ids inside `deck_versions.cards` and every game's deck and version (skipped, said aloud, on a pre-0018 dump).
- **Seventy-three mutation checks: seventy-one caught on the first run.** The two survivors had no pin: the gate's "no journal" branch (both games declare one — now pinned through a registry mock that drops the declaration) and `fitsSegmented`'s eight-segment bound (no option set reaches nine — now pinned directly: eight short labels fit, nine don't, six with a long one). Both caught on the rerun. Pins added before the run: History's saved-only count and Delete-less played rows, the version route's 409, PATCH's `null` clearing a detail.

### Y9b decisions and deviations from `WAVE4.md` D10 (2026-10-11, `f1e53f5`)

D10's Y9b half shipped: logging a game from a phone is "Log a game → Won → the toast" — two taps with the players remembered (three the first time a Magic table isn't 4; One Piece is always two). No migration, no route, no dependency; the route table is byte-identical. What the package decided:

- **The share page's door.** The owner's row leads with "Log a game" — **Log a game · Open in editor · Copy ▾ · Share… · Buy this deck** (pinned). "Phone first" is read as its place in the row everywhere: one DOM order, no CSS reordering. It shows wherever the game declares a journal (both do), for every owner: an account owner logs; a guest owner (this browser's claim token) gets the same door and the dialog reads "Sign in to keep a game journal" linked to `/account?next=/d/<publicId>` — back to the share page, not the editor — and sends nothing. **Account-only = no claim token in this browser** (inside the owner's row that means the session owns it — the editor's rule), not `isOwner` alone: the private gate's fetch reports `isOwner` true for a claim-token holder too (`isDeckOwner` matches the token), and would have shown a guest the log and then a 400 (found in review, pinned). The visitor row is byte-identical (its pins untouched; prod's visitor rows read the same after the deploy).
- **What it opens: Y9a's Games dialog itself**, not a lighter sheet — one dialog, one set of words, one place for Y10's numbers. So the share page's quick log shows the recent games and the allowance line too (owner-only, account-only, one GET of 4 statements per open). `onBeforeLog` answers true at once: the share page has no editor, so the saved list is the list played. `GamesDialog` gained `returnPath` (the sign-in sentence's `?next=`; the editor's stays the default).
- **Loaded on demand.** `log-game-door.tsx` holds one `next/dynamic(…, { ssr: false })` import of `log-game-dialog.tsx` (the dialog plus the toast, the tier and the adapter) that both new doors share. The owner row preloads the chunk on mount; `/account` preloads when either menu (⋯ or right-click) opens. Measured on the production build: the dialog's code lives in four chunks (15–30 KB, 5–10 KB gzipped), none among a visitor's share-page scripts (27 locally and on prod after the deploy); the share page's entry chunks went 21 / 1,283 KB → 20 / 1,274 KB and `/account`'s 19 / 1,221 → 19 / 1,213 KB. **Found:** the Turbopack dev server writes a `next/dynamic` chunk's script tag into every page's HTML — a dev artifact, absent from production. `SheetShell` moved to `sheet-shell.tsx` so the lazy chunk doesn't carry the swap sheets.
- **The toast** is the shared `showLoggedToast` (`logged-toast.ts` — the editor's `handleLogged` now calls it too): "Logged a win · Undo", Undo = the games route's DELETE. On the share page it rides the page's own Toaster, whose viewport now carries `data-game` like the editor's (the Undo button rings in the game's accent); on `/account` the page's one Toaster. The dialog closes first (a modal blocks a toast's Undo); focus returns to the door.
- **`/account`'s door.** "Log a game…" follows Copy share link in a group of its own (a separator above; a notebook icon) — the ⋯ list pinned as Open in editor · Open in new tab · View share page · Copy share link · **Log a game…** · Move to folder · Delete deck…. It opens the dialog right on `/account` (no navigation, no latch); the dialog renders beside the delete guard, outside the ContextMenu — React events bubble through portals, so a right-click inside the dialog must not reach the tile. No refresh after a log (nothing on the tile changes). `journalGame` is decided on the server (`AccountDeckMeta.journalGame`; null = no item), so the island imports no adapter until a log is asked for. The sign-in sentence can only show there on a 401 (every deck on `/account` is the session's); it returns to `/account`.
- **Opponents** (in "More details", after Seat; `opponents-field.tsx`):
  - The legend is the adapter's leader noun — "Opponents' commanders", "Opponent's leader" at a 1v1 table. Each picked opponent is a row of chips (the name and a remove button); one empty box asks for the next opponent while the table has room — at most pod − 1. "Add a second commander" under a picked opponent opens a box for the pair (one at a time) where the adapter's `opponentLeaders` is 2; One Piece names one leader, so no second box ever shows.
  - **The first box asks the `leaders` scope** (X2's hub scope, color chips on Magic rows, the printed id on One Piece rows). **Deviation in detail: the second box asks every card** (`cards` scope) — Magic's Backgrounds aren't leader candidates (Raised by Giants and Agent of the Iron Throne measured `is_leader_candidate = false`; 2 of 31 Backgrounds are), so the hub scope could never offer one. The route contract and the suggest bucket are X2's, unchanged.
  - **What's stored** is each pick's external key off the suggest row, in the order picked, a pair as one entry of two — Magic's Scryfall oracle id, One Piece's printed id (`smoke:games` posts real suggest rows and reads them back equal to `card_identities.external_key`).
  - **One Piece chips name the printing**: "Enel (OP15-058)" — the adapter's `idBadge` rule (17 printed Enels), the import format. Found in the dev pass: a bare "Enel" chip couldn't say which Enel was picked. Magic's names are unique, so its chips are the name alone.
  - **A name typed but never picked stops the log** ("Pick the commander from the list, or clear the box.") and unfolds the details to show the box — like a turn outside 1–99, said rather than sent (a typed name has no key). The details' Collapsible became controlled for it; the boxes' text survives folding.
  - Shrinking the table drops opponents past pod − 1, as it clears a seat past it.
  - Base UI fills a picked row's name into the box after the pick's own handler: each box is keyed by a serial the pick bumps, and the guarded updater drops the late write (pinned: the next box is empty after a pick).
  - The boxes are 44 px on touch like the dialog's other controls (X2's input is 32 px site-wide — the fix lives in the field, not in X2's component).
- **The route doesn't resolve opponents.** Y9a's routes are fenced for this package, and the box only offers rows whose keys pass the route's shape: every leader candidate does (measured: 4,076 Magic keys, longest 36 characters; 142 One Piece, longest 9; none fails `^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9-]*$`). The keys are shape-checked only (LATER row 198).
- **The recent games don't name opponents yet**: the games GET returns keys, not names (LATER row 199 — Y10's editable recent games are its trigger).
- **Fifty mutation checks: forty-four of the first forty-nine caught on the first run.** The five survivors had no pin — the same card picked twice as a pair, the second box staying open after its pick, Undo leaving its toast, and the focus return on both doors (jsdom closes a menu at once, so `/account`'s ⋯ path is pinned through the right-click menu, whose trigger is the tile) — all pinned and caught on the rerun. The share page's `accountDeck` rule changed after the run (above); its mutations were re-anchored and one more added (the gate's `isOwner` trusted again) — all caught.

### Y10 decisions and deviations from `WAVE4.md` D10 (2026-10-11, `694f67e`)

D10's Results paragraph shipped: the record tells the truth about small numbers. No migration, **no route — the route table byte-identical**; two response shapes grew (said before building): the games GET gains `record`, `lists` and `names`, the versions GET gains each version's `record` and `latestListHash`, and the games POST gains `first`. No dependency. What the package decided:

- **The numbers are counted on the server, worded on the client.** The games GET's `record` is one grouped statement — `select version, pod_size, result, count(*) … group by version, pod_size, result` over the deck's games — so the totals cover every game, not only the 50 the GET returns. A pure, isomorphic `src/lib/journal/record.ts` (`summarizeRecord`, `shownPercent`, `tooFewToCompare`, `tallyByVersion`) turns the cells into the dialog's groups; the thresholds are constants there (`PERCENT_FROM` 10, `COMPARE_FROM` 5, `RECORD_LISTS` 10) and the words live in `JOURNAL_COPY`.
- **Counts always, judgments gated.** Every group shows its games and wins ("won 3 of 7"); a percentage only from 10 games, in every group (the record line "13 games · 6–7 · won 46%", a pod row, a list row); a list with fewer than 5 games adds "Too few games to compare" on its own line. No streaks, no ratings.
- **W–L–D**: the draws appear only when there are some — "6–7", "5–3–1"; History's "Played 3 · 2–1" reads the same way. The notation is `aria-hidden` and a screen reader hears "6 wins, 7 losses" / "Played 3: 2 wins, 1 loss".
- **The pod split carries the table's baseline**: Magic rows "By players" — "4 players · won 6 of 12 · 50% · an even table wins 1 in 4", smallest table first; One Piece is always 1v1, so its record says "An even table wins 1 in 2." once and shows no split.
- **By list**: the newest 10 lists games were played with, newest first, each "since" the list played before it (the next older version with games — not the previous version number, which may never have been played), in `diffSummary`'s existing words — "v8, since v5: +2 · -2". **Deviation in detail**: D10's "+2 −2" is read as illustrative, not a new format — one diff line site-wide (History's Compare and the fork sections print the same). The first list played is its number alone ("v3"). The server reads at most 11 snapshots (one statement) and sends only the one-line summaries. Older lists are counted, not described: "and 1 earlier list".
- **"No list kept"** (LATER row 196): games logged at the played allowance count in every total and in the pod split, and sit in their own row under the lists — never folded into a version they weren't played with.
- **LATER row 199 fired and closed — names through the GET.** The games GET looks up the returned games' opponent keys in `card_identities` on `(game_id, external_key)` — one statement over the page's keys, in the deck's own game, index-served (`card_identities_game_external_key`) — and answers `names`. A recent game gains a second line: "vs Atraxa, Praetors' Voice; Kraum, Ludevic's Opus + Tymna the Weaver" ("; " between opponents — commander names hold commas, Y5's precedent; "+" inside a pair). One Piece names carry the printed id like the chips ("vs Enel (OP15-058)"). A key that names no card (row 198) shows as itself.
- **Recent games are editable.** Each of the ten recent lines has "Edit" (described by its line). The dialog swaps to an edit view: "Edit the game of Oct 9", "Played with v7 — an edit never changes the list." ("Logged with no list kept — an edit never changes that."), then the log's own fields filled with the game as stored — Result (a Segmented), Players, Date, Turn it ended, Bracket, Seat, the opponents as chips with names, Notes — the log's details component shared, so what the edit shows is what it saves. **Save sends only what changed** through PATCH (`null` clears a detail; untouched, nothing is sent); a smaller table drops the seat past it and the opponents past pod − 1 as the log does; a turn outside 1–99 or typed-but-unpicked text is said and not sent; the route's 400 is said in its words; a 401 turns the dialog to the sign-in sentence. **Delete game** asks first (`window.confirm`, like History) and uses Y9a's DELETE (the snapshot goes with its last game). After either, the list and the record load again, a polite line says "Saved the changes to that game." / "Deleted that game.", and focus returns to that game's Edit (to the Recent games heading after a delete). The edit's Players choice never changes the remembered pod.
- **History's rows** gain "Played 3 · 2–1" for every version a game was played with — saved and played alike — from the versions GET's per-version `record` (the same grouped statement, `tallyByVersion`). This is how History "groups played snapshots under their games" (D10): each list says its games; the games themselves stay in the Games dialog (no nested lists).
- **"No card changes since vN"**: the versions GET sends `latestListHash` — the newest version's playable-list hash; a newest version from before the hash (or under an older prefix) is hashed in the GET from its cards and **never stamped** (a GET writes nothing; the first log still stamps it). History hashes the screen with the isomorphic `playableListHash` and, when they match, says under Save version "No card changes since v8 (tags and printings aren't compared)." **Save version stays enabled**: a note, a tag or a printing change is still worth a version, and the hash doesn't see them. It compares the newest version only (D10's words).
- **The first logged game earns one restrained Warden line** — "The Warden keeps this deck's record from here." (`WARDEN_COPY.journal.firstGame`, pinned in `warden-copy.test.ts`; the no-"approve" guard covers it) — as the shared toast's description, so all three doors carry it. The POST answers `first` from one read under the deck lock just before the insert (`select id … limit 1`), so two logs can't both be first; after an Undo the next log is the first again. A first game is never capped (a played snapshot exists only while a game uses it), so the description slot never has to choose.
- **Nothing public**: no record on the share page, the tiles or the OG image; the games routes stay session + owner, the versions GET owner-only.
- **Measured** (dev, `DB_LOG`): the games GET 4 → 7 route statements with a record, lists and opponents (5 with none — the lists and names reads skip when empty); the versions GET 2 → 3 (+1 only for an unhashed newest version); the POST 7 → 8 when it reuses, 10 → 11 when it mints. In the database the grouped count and the names lookup run in under 0.1 ms (the names read index-served; the count seq-scans only because `deck_games` holds 15 rows today). Dev's 1.3–2.8 s wall times are round trips from the dev machine to Neon, as in Y9a.
- **Found by the checks**: (1) the baseline `pnpm check` exited 1 on an unhandled teardown error — Y9b's owner-row preload of the lazy Games dialog finishing after `private-share-gate.test.tsx`'s environment closed (a full-run timing flake; the file passes alone): the three test files whose components preload the dialog now import it up front, so the preload resolves from the module cache. (2) Writing History's RTL suite found Restore's notes saying "1 card no longer exist" and "1 chosen printing no longer exist" — the verb agrees now. (3) The dev pass found the 44 px Edit targets sitting low beside one-line games — the rows center.
- **HistoryDialog's RTL suite** (WAVE4 E's pin-matrix row; Y9a had added three): loading with the edit key and `no-store`, Save version (status, reload, the route's 409), Compare (the diff by name; "no card changes" for tags and printings), Restore (asked first; flushed, posted, re-hydrated; its notes; a 422), Delete (asked first; the 409 for a version a game uses), a fork's upstream, plus Y10's rows and line — 20 tests.
- **Fifty mutation checks, all fifty caught on the first run** — the gates (10 and 5, each off by one), the groups' order and rows, the no-list row, the diff's direction and its `since`, the names' game scope, `first`, the unhashed-newest rule, History's line and rows, every edit rule (only what changed, nothing when untouched, the reload, focus, names on the chips, the pod's stranded seat, a bad turn, the confirm, a 401, blank notes), the percentages in every group, "approve" slipping into a journal string, and the Warden line on the wrong game. Pins added before the run: a `logged-toast` unit test (the description per case) and the versions route's first test file.

## Addendum — Wave-5 supersessions (added 2026-10-12, U1 step 0)

The U-series (`WAVE5.md`, build plan §6f) implements the design lab's decisions (`DESIGN-LAB.md`, `e766299`) under the owner's 22 answers of 2026-10-12. Five of those answers amend statements in this contract, and U1 itself replaces four parts of the Button recipe that R1a inlined from shadcn's Nova style. As with the earlier addenda, the sections above, `WAVE2.md`, `WAVE3.md` and `WAVE4.md` stay as written; this addendum says which of their statements no longer hold:

- **F1 supersedes §1's "8 px controls."** The radius system is the one that ships, and no code moves for it: 12 px (`--radius`, `rounded-lg`) for panels, inputs and the default and large buttons; 9.6 px (`--radius-md`, `rounded-md`) for the small sizes and, from U1, every icon button; 16.8 px (`--radius-xl`) for dialogs. The lab measured every comp at these radii. Its Gate 6 ("One radius system (12 px panels, 8 px controls)", `DESIGN-LAB-session-prompt.md` §0.5) reads 12 / 9.6 / 16.8 from here on; that prompt is a session record and stays as written.
- **F4 supersedes §1's "eyebrow 12 uppercase tracking-wide (already the house style)."** The uppercase 12 px label names list groups only (the share page's and the editor's card groups). Page sections are serif h2s, as `WAVE2.md` D0 already says for site pages; home's four section eyebrows become serif h2s in U5.
- **F7 amends §1 Mechanics' motion policy.** The reduced-motion backstop still zeroes every transform and every duration, with one exception from U2: the dialog, the drawer, the toast and the save slot (four named `data-slot`s) cross-fade on opacity over 150 ms instead of popping, because reduced motion should mean a gentler fade, not an abrupt cut. `tokens.test.ts`'s backstop pin holds.
- **F8 amends §3's "Text-heavy panel interiors stay opaque enough for predictable reading."** Panel interiors stay opaque. From U3b, chrome (the editor header, the sticky search block, the phone tab bar and stuck group headers) may be the `--chrome` material, the background at 88 % with a blur, where content scrolls under it; under `prefers-reduced-transparency: reduce` and `prefers-contrast: more` the solid strip and the hairline come back. Drawers, the phone sheet, popovers and chips stay opaque.
- **F10 amends §4's "buttons respond in about 120 ms."** Still 120 ms (U1 brings the Button there from `transition-all`'s 150 ms). From U2 the press is a scale (0.98, or 0.96 on `xs` and `icon-xs`) with the hover color held, replacing the 1 px `translate-y-px` nudge; with reduced motion, the color alone.
- **U1 supersedes four parts of `src/components/ui/button.tsx`'s recipe.** The outline variant's plate (`border-border bg-background`, `dark:border-input dark:bg-input/30`) becomes a bare line on `--border-control` (the foreground at 55 % on ivory, 40 % on charcoal) with no fill of its own, because the lab measured the old edge at 1.28 to 1.49:1 on every surface. The ghost variant, which inherited its parent's color at rest, rests in `text-muted-foreground` and comes forward on hover. The `sm` label moves from 12.8 px (`text-[0.8rem]`) to 13 px. The per-size icon radii (`rounded-[min(var(--radius-md),10px)]` and `…12px)]` on the small sizes, the 12 px base on `icon` and `icon-lg`) become `rounded-md` on every icon size and on `xs` and `sm`. `transition-all` becomes a named list at 120 ms.

### U1 decisions and deviations from `WAVE5.md` D1 (2026-10-12, `27d3631`; step 0 `53be3ab`)

D1 shipped as drawn: the lab's D recipe with A's states and the ⊕ additions, the edge pair in a third `:root` / `.dark` block, Input / Select / the Segmented on the token. No migration, no route (the `pnpm build` route table byte-identical), no dependency. What the package decided:

- **The destructive steps (F16)**: light hover `/20` → `/15`; dark hover `/30` → `/20`, which lands on the dark rest tint, so **the dark rest stepped `/20` → `/15`** (the rule's second branch) and hover stays the heavier state. Measured live on dev with a real pointer hover, label over the tint, page / card: 4.63 / 4.97 (light), 5.05 / 4.53 (dark); at rest 5.02 / 5.40 and 5.54 / 4.97. `/10` was never needed. `aria-expanded` holds the hover tint (the measurement's static stand-in read the same numbers as the real hover).
- **The reason's API: `disabled` + `title`, no new prop.** `Button` passes `focusableWhenDisabled={focusableWhenDisabled ?? Boolean(disabled && title)}`, so an explicit `false` still opts out. Base UI 1.7.0 then renders `aria-disabled="true"`, no native `disabled`, keeps `tabIndex` 0, and drops clicks, pointerdowns and every key but Tab. Measured on dev: the button hovers (`:hover` matches, `cursor: not-allowed`, opacity 0.5), takes focus, and the accessibility tree reads it as disabled with the title as its description. A disabled Button with no title keeps the native attribute and `pointer-events: none`.
- **Converted call sites: one.** The autofill sheet's Retry during a 429 window carries "Too many requests. Retry works again after the wait." — its own sentence, not the visible message, because a control change during the lock can end in a connection error that replaces the 429 sentence while Retry stays locked. Its touch hint is the 429 sentence directly above it, so no new line was added (the on-touch default held without new markup). The other two sites the prompt named needed nothing: the Suggestions "Only cards I own" control is a native checkbox whose `<label>` already carries the reason as its `title` plus the "import a collection" link as visible text, and the Fit sheet has no disabled Retry (its "Try again" is an inline text button that is never disabled). Two Buttons already passed `disabled` + `title` (Fork while busy, the Combo Radar's "Suggest full list" while busy); their titles describe the action, and under the new API they stay focusable while busy, so focus is not dropped mid-request. No test pinned their native attribute.
- **Deviation: the Segmented's line-height is set.** D1 said `py-1 text-[0.8125rem]`; an arbitrary font size sets no line-height, so the height would have followed whatever the surrounding text inherits (19.5 px under the body's 1.5 → a 30 px control). The items are `py-1 text-[0.8125rem] leading-4.5`: 18 + 8 + the 1 px edge × 2 = **28 px on every surface** (measured on the editor, the share page and the hub), 46 px on touch (44 px items inside the edge). The thumb is `duration-[120ms]`, `ease-out` until U2.
- **Toggle / ToggleGroup: parity now.** The Toggle's outline flavour reads the token. Nothing renders it today (the Segmented uses the default flavour and draws its own container edge), so the first package that does inherits the measured line. The Select trigger has no call site in the app either; both are pinned in `src/components/ui/control-edge.test.tsx`.
- **`lg` keeps D's `px-3.5 gap-2`** (no call site yet; U5's hero is the first).
- **Ghosts**: the 40 ghost call sites were read for a color they inherit on purpose. None has one; the red "Delete game" in the Games dialog sets `text-destructive` itself and still wins through tailwind-merge (pinned).
- **tailwind-merge 3.6 reads `border-(--border-control)` as a border color**: it drops the base's `border-transparent` and keeps a width like `border-2` (checked before relying on it; pinned through `cn()` in `button.test.tsx`).
- **Measured on dev** (the Appendix B helper, alpha composited over the real ancestors; Brave through the DevTools MCP; a throwaway `src/app/(site)/qa-u1/` page for the variant matrix, the size strip and the Share dialog without a deck row, deleted before the build and never committed). Before, page / card: the outline edge 1.48 / 1.34 (dark) and 1.27 / 1.38 (light), the Segmented's container 1.48 / 1.54 at 22 px. After: every token edge 3.50 / 3.48 (dark) and 3.36 / 3.44 (light), and 3.38 / 3.46 on the Share dialog's popover surface; outline, Input, Select and the Segmented alike. Labels at rest ≥ 4.5:1 on every surface measured (the ghost at rest 8.47 / 7.71 dark, 6.07 / 6.55 light). The size strip (six variants × eight sizes = 48, where the lab's 40 was five recipes × eight) all ≥ 44 × 44 at 390 with touch; the editor at 390 with touch 268 buttons, none under 44, no overflow; the share page, the hub and home at 390 the same. The editor's deck-pane controls at 1440: 28 px only (the three Segmenteds, Draw sample hand, Keep this deck), the row steppers 24 (the size map's `icon-xs` inside a 28 px row). The served CSS carries both declarations and the `border-(--border-control)`, `duration-[120ms]`, `leading-4.5` and `text-[0.8125rem]` rules.
- **Found in passing**: `autofill-sheet.tsx` holds a literal NUL byte inside `pickKey`'s template (one of the tree's four); an exact-string edit anchored on that line missed silently and was re-anchored. The light theme's white mana pips losing their circle on ivory (LATER row 206) shows on every editor screenshot.

### U2 decisions and deviations from `WAVE5.md` D2 (2026-10-12, `95ea360`)

D2 shipped in one session (no U2b split: the ten `ui/` primitives were one mechanical token map). No migration, no route (the `pnpm build` route table byte-identical), no dependency added. What the package decided:

- **Plain `@theme`, not `@theme inline`, for the four curves** (D2 said inline). The utilities then read `var(--ease-*)`, and the variables exist at `:root` for the `--animate-dw-*` shorthands and hand-written rules (verified live: all four at `:root`, `linear()` parsed, Button and the Segmented thumb computing `linear(0 0%, 0.132 8.33%…)` at 120 ms). The same block resets `--ease-*: initial` (no `ease-out` utility can be written any more) and sets the default transition to 120 ms on settle-120, so every un-tuned `transition-colors` lands in the control slot. `--animate-dw-*` and `--animate-ring-glow` stay in `@theme inline` beside the keyframes.
- **`dw-in` / `dw-out` read Tailwind's own `--tw-duration` / `--tw-ease`** (registered, not inherited), so a site writes `animate-dw-in duration-400 ease-settle-400` in the house idiom; the default is 250 ms on settle-250. The parameters `--dw-opacity` (0 = a fade; the qty pop sets 1), `--dw-scale`, `--dw-x`, `--dw-y` are `@property`-registered with `inherits: false`, so a popup's scale never reaches an animated child. A popup's `[--dw-scale:0.95]` is unprefixed so the exit zooms like the entry (one token both ways); `slide-in-from-*-2` became `data-[side=…]:[--dw-x|y:±0.5rem]`. Base UI's unmount still waits on the exit (measured: a menu's `dw-out` ran 250 ms and the popup left the DOM at ~290 ms). With no `animate-in` left, the `tw-animate-css` import left `globals.css` (the package stays in `package.json`, LATER row 218).
- **Durations by D2's table**: popups, dialogs and their backdrops 250 / settle-250; the drawer 400 / settle-400 (overlay and popup; the content cross-fade 250; the handle 250); the toast 400 / settle-400 on transform and opacity, height 120 / settle-120, and **flick-400 only on `data-ending-style` + `data-swipe-direction`** (a thrown toast); the Warden shield 400 / settle-400, its line 250; the qty pop, the ambient crop and copy status 250; the dealt hand 200 per card on settle-400 (D2's number); chevrons, the mark's tilt, the tab indicator and the Segmented thumb 120 / settle-120; the analytics bars settle-400; the unused `sheet.tsx` and `navigation-menu.tsx` brought onto the tokens too (250, chevron 120). **The drawer's release is settle-400 both ways**; flick-400 on a swipe release needs U4b's `data-swiping` split (left to U4b, as the prompt's default).
- **The count-up** samples settle-400 in JavaScript: `tokens.ts` exports the curve's 21 points (`settle400`, pinned equal to the stylesheet) and `sampleLinear`. D2 said "a 13-point lookup"; settle-400 has 21 points, and the same curve was the stated intent, so it uses all 21.
- **The ring glow** is a halo `<span>` (a 3 px accent ring, `opacity-0` at rest) whose opacity and `scale` animate on settle-400 — an HTML element rather than D2's second `<circle>`, because Chrome does not composite animations on SVG elements; the trace check is the acceptance. `completion-ring.test.tsx` re-pins the class on the halo.
- **The press**: `motion-safe:active:scale-[0.98]` in the base, `0.96` on `xs` / `icon-xs` (tailwind-merge keeps only the size's), each variant's hover look held on `:active` token for token (pinned: every `active:` class has its `hover:` twin; destructive `/15` light, `/20` dark), `translate-y-px` and the `haspopup` exclusion gone. The Segmented items take the press (scale + `active:text-foreground`); **the filter chips wait for U10b's Chip** (LATER row 219). No call site overrides its variant's hover tint in a way the held press would contradict (grepped).
- **The reduced-motion exception names five slots, not four**: `dialog-content`, `alert-dialog-content` (the default — a dialog is a dialog), `drawer-popup`, `toast`, and the save slot's **children and their `::before`** (`[data-slot="save-slot"] *`, the real name; D2 said `save-status`). It sets transitions to opacity-only at 150 ms linear, animations to 150 ms linear, and pins the `dw` parameters to a plain fade. **Deviation: no `transform: none` / `translate: none` / `scale: none`** — the dialogs centre by `translate`, the drawer follows the finger by `transform` and the toasts stack by `transform`, so zeroing them would un-place all three. Instead the drawer and a toast fade in where they land, and on the way out their move to the closed position waits (`transition: opacity 150ms, transform 0s 150ms`) so they fade where they stood. `tokens.test.ts` pins the block and that no `none` is written.
- **Proving reduced motion**: the DevTools MCP has no reduced-motion switch, so the stylesheet's own `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)` rules were copied out of the CSSOM at runtime, unwrapped, into a `@layer base` style element on a throwaway `qa-u2` page (deleted before the build, never committed). Measured: dialog and alert dialog `dw-in` / `dw-out` 150 ms from `scale(1)` (no movement), still at `translate: -50% -50%`; the drawer's opacity 0 → 1 over ~150 ms with its transform at identity throughout, and on close the same in reverse before it unmounted; a new toast fading in place; the save slot's two layers at 150 ms with scale pinned; a Button's transition 0.01 ms.
- **The save slot**: the new label fades in (`animate-dw-in`) while the outgoing words fade out on a `::before` that reads `data-was` with empty alt text (`content: attr(data-was) / ""`), so the slot's `textContent` is still the one live label (every text and `data-status` pin held) and the outgoing words are never announced; the outgoing layer is text only (a leaving "Saved" drops its check for those 250 ms). The first paint doesn't fade. "Saving…" is shown once a save has run `SAVING_LABEL_AFTER_MS` = 300 ms (a flag the effect's timer sets; cleared in the cleanup); before that a saving slot reads "Unsaved…". `h-7 w-36` unchanged. The face carries no `dw-*` class at first paint, so the prod check reads the classes in the served CSS and on the editor's menus rather than on the resting slot.
- **Toasts**: `ToastProvider` defaults `limit` to 2; a third toast is marked `data-limited` and fades (measured).
- **Measured on dev** (Brave through the DevTools MCP; the editor on the Giada precon draft, state-only): a 390 px / 4× CPU trace shows no non-composited animation — the CLS culprit insight names no animation root cause (the remaining 0.07 shift is the header's slot problem U3a owns), and every running animation (the ring glow, three `dw-in`) is opacity + transform only; the drawer settles inside 400 ms (popup and overlay 400 ms on settle-400, unmounted at ~418 ms); the toast at 400 ms; menus at 250 on settle-250. U1's measured edges and labels were not re-run: U2 changes no color declaration (each held `active:` class duplicates its `hover:` twin, pinned), so U1's numbers stand.
- **Found in passing**: Tailwind v4 scans the Markdown docs, so prose that quotes a class (the old toast transition, `drop-shadow`, `ease-[cubic-bezier(…)]`) mints dead CSS into the bundle (LATER row 217). A stale `.next/dev/types/validator.ts` referencing the deleted `qa-u2` page failed the first `pnpm build`; deleting `.next/dev` fixed it.

### U8 decisions and deviations from `WAVE5.md` D3 (2026-10-12, `948cb0a`)

D3 shipped in one session: the ladder, the skip link, `aria-current`, the eased theme change and the tinted hero shadow. No migration, no route (the `pnpm build` route table byte-identical), no dependency, no font (`opsz` stays `auto`; no `font-variation-settings`). What the package decided:

- **The ladder is thirteen classes in `@layer components`**, one rule each, every rule setting the face, size, leading, tracking and weight (pinned value for value in `tokens.test.ts`). D3's nine steps, plus three that D3 described but didn't name: `.type-label` is three classes (`.type-label` 14 / 20, `.type-label-sm` 13 / 18 / +0.005em, `.type-label-xs` 12 / 16 / +0.01em, all 500), whose leadings D3 left open (Tailwind's text-sm and text-xs pairs; 18 is U1's Segmented line); `.type-meta-multiline` is D3's "multi-line: 12 / 18"; `.type-eyebrow` is meta's uppercase form (12 / 16 / +0.025em / 500, `text-transform: uppercase`), byte-for-byte the eyebrow every list-group label writes today as utilities. The face is part of each step (`var(--font-display)` or `var(--font-sans)`; Tailwind emits `--font-display` at `:root` because the rule references it). Leading is absolute rem, and a step with zero tracking writes `letter-spacing: 0`, so an inherited tracking never leaks in. Verified live: a utility beside a ladder class still wins (`type-title tracking-tight text-sm` computes −0.35 px and 14 / 20 with the class's 600), which is why the converted sites carry none.
- **Converted**: the home H1 → `.type-display`; **all eighteen** page h1s → `.type-title` (D3 said ten; every `text-3xl tracking-tight` line was a page h1, the share page's included, its class pin moved), `global-error.tsx`'s too, since it imports `globals.css` (it declares no next/font variables, so its face falls back to the inherited sans exactly as before; only the tracking changes); the serif h2s → `.type-section` on the hubs (7 on `/c/`, 3 on `/l/`), the card page (2 and the printings table's) and, beyond D3's list, `/privacy` (6), `/legal` (6), `/account`'s section titles and the `/cards` set view's "most played" heading, all of them already Literata 18 / 600, so only their leading moves (28 → 26); the precons and sets year headings → `.type-small`; the Warden line → `.type-caption` on its `<p>` (before, the line was Geist 12 / 16 with only the words' span in Literata; now the whole line is Literata 13 / 20 / 500, the shield and the approval action's 22 px indent unchanged); the bracket line → `.type-meta-multiline` (the lab's "multi-line meta (hints, bracket) 12 / 18"); `.type-micro` on the ambient and `SurfaceHeader` credit chips, the editor's attribution line, the analytics captions (bucket values and labels, the histogram legend, the stat tone, both hints) and the sample hand's alt-text size. Not converted: the three 24 px page h1s and the 20 px `/cards` set h2 (no ladder step at either size; LATER row 220), home's game h2s (U5), the uppercase eyebrows (U3a, U5 and U6 take theirs; LATER row 221 holds the rest), EmptyState titles (16, D3) and the wordmark (D3). D3's two `tabular-nums` misses were already covered (the search rows' `×N` carries it; the Like count is a Button, U1).
- **Acceptance grep** (bare `tracking-*` in non-test `.tsx`): 59 lines → 40. `tracking-tight` 25 → 6: the four no-step headings above, home's game h2 (U5) and the wordmark. `tracking-wide` stays 32: 26 eyebrow-form headings and six non-headings (home's "Open beta" pill, U5's to retire, and five collapsible triggers). `tracking-widest` 1 (the menu shortcut) and `tracking-normal` 1 (the bracket sheet's count resetting its eyebrow's tracking).
- **The skip link**: `SkipLink`, the first child of the root layout's `<body>`, so the editor routes have it too. Its target is `#main`, and **every `<main>` carries `id="main"` as its first attribute** (30 of them, pinned by a source scan in `skip-link.test.tsx`). **The editor's panes became its `<main>`** (the Tabs root, `render={<main id="main" />}`): WAVE5 listed the editor's `<main>` under U3a, and the skip link needed a target there first. Once hydrated, a click (Enter) sets `tabindex="-1"` on main, focuses it and drops the tabindex on blur, so main never joins the tab order and a mouse click inside it never focuses it. The default is prevented, so the URL keeps no `#main`; before hydration the plain fragment works. `#main:focus { outline: none }`: a page-sized ring says nothing. Measured: one Tab from the page start lands on it on home, `/commanders`, `/decks/new` and the editor at 1440 and 390. It shows as a 32 px pill at the top-left (the control edge, the 3 px ring, `.type-label`, the first hand-rolled control to use the class). Enter moves focus to main, and the next Tab reaches "Build a deck" on home and the Deck pane on the phone editor.
- **`aria-current="page"`, exact paths**: Build on `/decks/new`, My decks on `/account`, and **both the Browse trigger and the open menu's entry** on its six indexes. The phone menu marks its entries the same way, with no visual change inside menus. `navLinkClass` gains `aria-[current=page]:text-foreground` (on charcoal, `#a9b0ad` → `#f3f1ea`). Hubs, card, event and deck pages name nothing (LATER row 223). Build and My decks became client islands (`usePathname`, not request data); measured live, a client navigation from `/commanders` to `/account` moves the mark from Browse to My decks; the route table didn't move.
- **The theme ease, deviation: body's background only.** D3 said `html`, `background-color` and `color`. Body, because body paints the canvas (html has no background of its own). The background alone, because measured on the editor at 4× CPU, easing the inherited text color restyled every element on every frame (45 to 51 ms frames, 9 to 10 in 400 ms) and made each element with its own color transition restart it each frame (2,274 transition starts in one switch), while the background alone ran at 12 to 13 ms frames, the instant flip's own cost. `disableTransitionOnChange` stays: next-themes' injected `transition: none !important` is unlayered, and an `!important` inside a cascade layer outranks it, so body's one rule gets through while everything else still changes at once. The gate is `data-theme-ready`, which `ThemeProvider`'s effect sets after a style read once hydrated (next-themes' own mount re-apply runs first, as a child's effect). Measured: a light-stored hard load starts **no** transition on html or body (a `transitionrun` recorder from document start); a switch starts exactly **one**, body's background-color at 250 ms on settle-250, ending 261 to 267 ms after the flip through about 20 interpolated colors. Before / after traces of one switch on the editor at 4× CPU: style recalc 106 → 115 ms, paint 6 → 12 ms, the flip frame unchanged (two full recalcs of about 2,200 elements at 50 to 56 ms each, inherent to a class flip). Reduced motion (U2's CSSOM-copy method, with the `no-preference` block removed, since it never matches under `reduce`): instant, no transition, body's duration the backstop's 0.01 ms. Cards, panels, borders and text change at once over the easing canvas (LATER row 222).
- **The hero card's shadow**: `HERO_CARD_SHADOW` (`shadow-lg shadow-primary/25 dark:shadow-black/60`) beside `SURFACE_BAND` in `surface-header.tsx`, used by the `/c/` and `/l/` hubs **and the card page's printing hero**. D3 named the hub; `/l/` is the other hub (the prompt's "`l/[slug]` has none" was a slip), and the card page's hero is the same card on the same band. Computed: the primary at 25 % on ivory, black at 60 % on charcoal, on both `shadow-lg` layers.
- **Measured on dev and prod** (Brave through the DevTools MCP; the stale automation Brave from an earlier session was quit first, with the owner's OK). The Warden line is Literata 13 / 20 / +0.13 px / 500, served from the variable font: one latin woff2 for the 200 to 900 `wght` range, so 500 is a real instance, not synthesis. Its contrast is 4.85:1 on ivory and 9.78:1 on charcoal (the Appendix B helper). Weight and size don't move a WCAG ratio, so D3's "lifts the light pair from 4.88:1" is legibility, not the number. Heading geometry against prod (pre-U8) at 1440 and 390 on home, a commander hub, a leader hub, a card page, `/precons`, `/sets`, `/privacy` and a share page: no heading changed its line count, only the ladder's own leading and tracking moved (the home H1 144 → 150 px over three lines at 390, descenders clear; section h2s 28 → 26; titles −0.75 → −0.45 px). On the editor, the status block is 5 px taller (the Warden line 16 → 20, the bracket line 20 → 21), and the attribution and credit chip keep their lines.

### U3a decisions and deviations from `WAVE5.md` D4 (2026-10-12, `ed8991f`)

D4's bones shipped in one session: the fixed slots, the status slot, the group headings on space, the 14 px qty and 11.2 px pips, the one-line controls row, `content-visibility`, the leader image's `priority`. No migration, no route (the `pnpm build` route table byte-identical), no dependency. What the package decided:

- **The status slot is derived from the lines as shipped** (the prompt's first option), not D4's `min-h-10` / `leading-5`: the Warden line (`.type-caption`, 20 px) or the progress line, then the bracket line (`.type-meta-multiline` 12 / 18 with its 16 px gauge on the baseline, 21 px) at `mt-1.5`, 47 px in all at 1440 and 390. The progress line and the problem toggle take `leading-5`, so the first line is 20 px in every state on a fine pointer (the toggle keeps its 44 px on touch, so a problems state is taller there; it arrives only with an edit), and every first-line dot sits centered in the 16 px icon gutter (`GutterDot`), so all the lines' text starts at one x. The bracket line is **present and empty before a read**: the same `<p>` and classes with an invisible gauge and one blank line, `data-status="empty"`, `aria-hidden`, no tabindex, nothing to press; the read lands in the same box (measured: 47 px before and after the seed, the leader zone unmoved). The editor passes `slot` to both components; the share page passes neither and keeps its markup.
- **"Why?" and "Retry" keep the pinned `pointer-coarse:min-h-11`** and stop growing the line on touch: `pointer-coarse:-my-[13px]` gives back the 26 px that reach past the 18 px line (the lab's phone comp used an `::after` hit area; the test pins the class). Measured at 390 with touch: the button 44 × 44, the line 21 px, the "Checking combos…" → read change moving nothing. They are muted at 500 and come forward on hover and focus (`transition-colors`, U2's 120 ms default); the underline is gone.
- **The bracket number**: `bracketLineLead` (pure, `line-view.ts`) takes the adapter line's words before its first ` · ` or ` — ` (the adapter puts the number first: "Bracket 1–2", "At least Bracket 3 (Upgraded)", "Your target: Bracket 2") and the line sets them in the foreground at 500, the rest muted; core's own lines ("Checking combos…", "Couldn't check combos") have no lead. The words and the line's text are unchanged (every text pin held).
- **The primary slot is fixed from `md`** (`md:w-[7.5rem]` `md:shrink-0` `md:justify-end`) and `display: contents` on phones. Keep this deck and Share never show together (Keep needs `liveDeckId === null`, Share needs `share`, which is only ever set with a live deck id), so one slot holds both. On phones the cluster (save 144 + slot + More 44 + Appearance 44) would overflow the header at 375 and 360 px for every saved deck; the phone header is U4a's (LATER row 228). Measured at 1440: Keep is 112 px in its 120 px slot, and the cluster, the save slot and More sit at the same x before and after the seed. Tools needed no slot (it renders unconditionally at `md`, one width). At 768 to 782 px the reserved slot wraps the cluster to the header's second row for every deck (LATER row 229, until U3b hides the meta).
- **The summary row**: the right cluster is `md:flex-nowrap`, the ownership line `min-w-0 truncate`, the count and the over-limit link `shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap`; phones keep the wrap (a nowrap cluster overflows 390 in the over-limit plus collection state; the phone summary is U4a's). On phones the row is held at "Add cards"' 44 px (`max-md:pointer-coarse:min-h-11`) and its left group stretches to the row (`self-stretch`): a centered group moved its own box 12 px when the button arrived (0.0027 of the 390 score, found by the layout-shift entries).
- **The leader zone while a URL seed is on its way** (`seeding` = `draftSeed !== null && !seedSettled`): the card's box as a `Skeleton` (the 488 / 680 aspect at the leader's width, the name row at the Remove button's 24 / 44 px, the caption's line), `aria-hidden`, measured identical to the seeded card's box (160 × 266.5 at 1440). The analytics and the sample hand wait for the seed too: pushed below the fold by the list, they were part of the 1440 shift. The deck's own empty state still shows meanwhile, as before.
- **The Tools pane (deviation, outside D4's list)**: with the header and the deck pane fixed, 0.0113 of the 1440 CLS remained, all of it the Tools pane's attribution pushed off screen when the seeder previews the commander. The pane is a column at `wide` (`wide:flex wide:flex-col`) and the attribution `mt-auto`, so it rests on the pane's bottom edge and slides only from there (1440: 0.0114 → 0.0020). Block flow in the drawer and the phone's Tools tab, so nothing changes there.
- **Group headings (`GroupHeading`, shared by the text and grid views and the leader zone)**: the h3 is `flex items-baseline gap-2 pt-5 pb-1.5`, the label `.type-eyebrow text-muted-foreground` (U8's class, today's exact look), the count `text-foreground text-sm font-semibold tabular-nums` (`data-slot="group-count"`); the section's `mt-4` is gone, the heading's padding is the space (the lab's comp). The leader zone's heading is the same component; the sample hand's `border-b pb-1` became `pb-1.5` under its `mt-6`. The sticky backing stays `bg-background` until U3b's `--chrome`; at 46 px it shows at rest as a band over the ambient art in dark.
- **The share page keeps today's look until U6**: `ruled` on `DeckTextView`, `DeckGridView`, `LeaderZone` and `SampleHand` (the hairline headings with the count inside, the 12 px qty, the text-xs pips at 0.72em) and no `slot` on its Warden and bracket lines. Verified on prod (`/d/jhr5ax43ewx7`): the headings ruled at 1 px, the qty 12 px, the DON!! chip 8.64 px, the hand's hairline, no `content-visibility`. U6 drops `ruled` (D7 takes this header and these rows).
- **Rows**: the qty is `text-sm font-medium` (14 / 500) in the same `w-6` cell. **Pips, deviation: scoped, not global.** `.pip` and `.don-cost` read `var(--pip-size, 0.72em)`, and the editor's `text-sm` pips column sets `--pip-size: 0.8em`: 11.2 px glyphs in 15.12 px circles (D4's "18.9 px circles" and the lab's "16.2 px" today multiplied the em by the column's size; the em is the pip's own), the DON!! chip 16.8 px, both on one center in a mixed row (measured; the row stays 28 px). D4 named the global rule, but `.pip` also draws the hubs' identity pips, the leader index, the card page, the Card pane, the search rows and the share page (LATER row 227). `.don-cost` follows `.pip`, its own comment's invariant (one scale, one baseline). Giada's widest cost is four pips (65 px, inside `min-w-20`); five- and six-pip costs (81 / 97 px) extend to the left with right edges aligned.
- **The controls row**: "View" is the group's `ariaLabel` with no visible label; Group and Sort keep theirs. It was already one line at 1200 and 1440 since U1's 28 px Segmenteds (471 of 536 px at 1200); dropping the label adds margin. The pane's toolbar controls measure 28 px only at 1200 and 1440 (View, Group, Sort, Draw sample hand); the row steppers are the size map's 24 px `icon-xs` in 28 px rows.
- **`content-visibility: auto` kept** on the editor's group sections (with `stickyHeaders`), each with a placeholder height from CSS (`--group-rows`: a 50 px heading and 28 px rows, 48 on touch, `contain-intrinsic-size: auto …`), measured equal to the drawn heights (246 / 974 / 358 / 246 / 190 / 190 px; scroll height 2,826 px with and without). A heading still sticks while its section is partly scrolled off (top 0 with the section 100 px above the pane), and `scrollIntoView` lands on a row in a skipped group. `window.find` finds text inside a skipped group but never scrolls the nested pane, with or without `content-visibility` (its own limit); the browser's find bar can't be driven through DevTools.
- **The preload: `priority` alone** (the prompt's default): `/decks/[id]/edit` has no server loader to emit it from, and a new read would put a private deck's commander into the HTML (LATER row 225). The leader image is eager, `fetchpriority="high"` and unfaded; the share page's stays lazy.
- **Search rows**: unselected rows take `active:bg-accent active:text-accent-foreground`, the selection's look.
- **Measured** (Brave through the DevTools MCP, the lab's throttle: 4x CPU, Fast 4G, dark, Giada's precon draft). CLS: prod 1440 0.2355 → 0.0020 and 390 0.0849 → 0.0074; dev 1440 0.2356 → 0.0020, 1200 0.0029, 390 0.0851 → 0.0075 (layout-shift entries; the traces agree). The rest at 390 is the phone header (row 228); at 1440, the attribution's slide below the fold and two search-hint keycaps. **LCP: D4's 1.0 s is not met** on the seeded draft: prod 390 2,190 → 1,923 ms (render delay 491 → 155 ms; the 1,660 ms load delay is the client-side seed chain, row 224); prod 1440 2,090 → 2,229 / 2,286 ms, where the LCP element is the Card tab's lazy, fading copy and its `onLoad` commit landed behind a ~170 ms script task in both runs (row 226). Dev LCP isn't comparable: dev's precon read lands at ~4 s, and before U3a the images' fades fell after the traces ended. The header and pane before and after the seed (the precon fetch gated in the page): identical boxes for the cluster, the save slot, More, the status slot and the leader section. Contrast (the Appendix B math on the changed pairs, dark / light): the bracket lead, the count and the qty 16.66 / 12.9:1; the muted rest, "Why?" and the labels 8.57 / 6.04:1; the Warden line 9.72 / 4.91:1.

### U3b decisions and deviations from `WAVE5.md` D4 (2026-10-13, `9a4a9e2`)

D4's materials shipped in one session: `--chrome` and its two fallbacks, the four chrome sites, the stuck gate with the 12 px gradient, the raised leader card, the header's name floor. No migration, no route (the `pnpm build` route table byte-identical), no dependency. What the package decided:

- **Where `--chrome` lives: a `:root` block of its own** after the edge pair, not inside it (`tokens.test.ts` pins that pair to one declaration a block), holding the material's four parameters: `--chrome` (the fill), `--chrome-backdrop` (`blur(12px) saturate(150%)`, which is Tailwind's backdrop blur md plus a backdrop saturate of 150; D4's "saturate-150" is the element's own filter, which would saturate the chrome's text), `--chrome-edge` (the stuck heading's hairline, transparent) and `--chrome-fade` (the top of its gradient). One declaration each, theme-neutral: the theme class sits on `<html>`, the `:root` element itself, so `var(--background)` resolves per theme where they are declared (computed on dev: `color-mix(in oklab, #0f1314 88%, transparent)` on charcoal, the ivory's on light; no local `--background` exists below `<html>`). Each fallback is one `:root` rule under its `@media`, after the base block: the fill `var(--background)`, the backdrop `none`, the edge `var(--border)`, the fade `transparent`. The built CSS keeps that order behind Lightning CSS's `@supports (color-mix)` fallback, so a browser without `color-mix` gets the solid strip. The four sites read it through `CHROME_CLASS` (`src/components/chrome-material.ts`: `bg-(--chrome) backdrop-filter-(--chrome-backdrop)`), real utilities, so tailwind-merge drops the tab list primitive's `bg-transparent`; Tailwind writes the `-webkit-` prefix beside the filter.
- **Deviation: oklab, not D4's oklch.** `color-mix(in oklch, var(--background) 88%, transparent)` computes `oklch(0.1829 0.0066 none / 0.88)` in Chromium: a `none` hue with the charcoal's chroma, which paints at hue 0. Measured on a canvas, the oklch chrome over the page's own color is 21,17,19 where the page is 14,18,20 (a magenta cast on every chrome strip) and the ivory 248,243,243 where it is 246,244,239; oklab and sRGB paint the true color at 88 %. U1's `--border-control` carries the same cast, small at a 1 px line (LATER row 231).
- **The header takes the material and keeps its hairline.** Nothing scrolls under it (each pane is its own scroller), so its material sits only over the art's veiled top, where it reads as the same surface as the search block below it; the hairline stays because the panes' vertical rules meet it and there is no scroll edge for a fade to replace. Under the fallbacks it is the solid strip with the same hairline. The tab bar keeps its top hairline (the lab's phone comp did); the search block had none and has none.
- **The stuck gate is an `IntersectionObserver` on each group's section: not a sentinel, and not `scroll-state()`.** A probe in Brave before any code (the Giada draft's six groups, `content-visibility` on): a zero-height sentinel as each section's first child went stale on jumps (after a jump from inside the Artifact group back to the top it still reported Artifact stuck), because one jump across a group taller than the pane carries a sentinel from one side of the pane to the other without its intersection ever changing. The section itself, always laid out (`content-visibility` skips a section's contents, never its own box), observed against a 1 px strip just above the pane's top edge (`rootMargin: "1px 0px -100% 0px"`, thresholds 0 and 1e-6, so a section that only touches the strip, its heading exactly at its own place, is not stuck), changes state whenever its heading sticks or comes loose, however far the jump. `scroll-state(stuck: top)` was exact in Brave for the visible heading too (it also flags headings pushed out above the pane, which paint nothing), but with it the measuring browser would exercise one path while Safari and Firefox ran the other; one path everywhere instead (LATER row 234 keeps the alternative). The state is React's: the observer's callback sets it and only that heading re-renders.
- **The gate, measured on the shipped code**: a stepped scroll through the whole list (54 steps at 1440, 48 at 1200) left 0 frames where the flagged heading differed from the one whose section straddles the pane's top; every jump landed right (top to the end, inside Creature to the Artifact group, back to the top; Creature's top exactly at the pane's top flags nothing, one pixel more flags Creature); the grid view 36 steps, 0 wrong. The one difference from that geometry is invisible: a group stays flagged at the instant its section's bottom touches the pane's top, when its heading is wholly above the pane and the gradient below it is clipped by the section's paint containment.
- **At rest the band is gone; stuck, the material.** `STICKY_HEADER_CLASS` is `sticky top-0 z-10`, no background. Stuck (`data-stuck`), the heading takes `CHROME_CLASS` and `shadow-[inset_0_-1px_0_var(--chrome-edge)]` (the fallback hairline as an inset shadow, so the 46 px heading and U3a's placeholders never move: 246 / 974 / 358 / 246 / 190 / 190 px after, as before) and a 12 px gradient span after the count (`from-(--chrome-fade)` to transparent at `top-full`, `aria-hidden` and empty), rendered only while stuck, so it never exists at rest; the label and the count stay the heading's first two children (U3a's pin). **The gradient and the material appear instantly**, no transition: a fade would show rows under a half-clear heading in a fast scroll. Reduced motion: nothing moves, so nothing to reduce. The leader zone's heading never sticks; the share page passes no `stickyHeaders`.
- **The fallbacks, measured** with U2's CSSOM-copy method (the served stylesheet's own two media rules copied into a style element at runtime, a heading stuck): the header, the search block and the stuck heading solid `rgb(15, 19, 20)` with no backdrop filter, the stuck heading's hairline back as `rgb(43, 52, 55)` inset (the panel hairline), its gradient transparent to transparent, the resting headings still bare; removing the copy restored the material. jsdom pins both media rules and the base block in `tokens.test.ts`.
- **Contrast**, computed for the worst case (dense row text straight under a heading's label, no blur at all): the muted label 6.23:1 on charcoal and 4.87:1 on ivory, the count 12.19 and 10.37; over the art alone (white or black art at the ambient's 8 / 4 % under the veil's thinnest stop, then the material) 8.34 and 6.03. Every pair clears 4.5:1, and the blur only softens the rows further.
- **The tab bar keeps its blur** (E's risk). Traced at 390 with 4x CPU while a script scrolled the Deck tab 12 px a frame down and back, with the blur and with the bar's blur switched off in the page, twice in each order: 320 frames presented and 1 or 2 dropped either way, rAF p99 13.5 / 11.1 ms with it against 13.0 / 10.9 without, the GPU's draw stage 0.14 to 0.17 ms a frame heavier. With the blur some frames waited one vsync between the compositor's frame and the draw (receive-to-draw p90 8.15 ms against 0.07): latency, not jank, on a desktop GPU. A real low-end phone is LATER row 232's, for U4b's real-phone checks.
- **The leader card, raised from `md`** (`LeaderZone raised`, the editor only). The row is the card and its facts side by side, one leader to a line; the card 180 px wide (180 × 250.81 at every tier from 768 up); the facts column beside it holds the name at 14 / 500 with Remove right after it, then one `.type-meta` muted line, `leaderFacts`: the adapter's type line and the caption, joined as the caption already joins its parts ("Legendary Creature — Angel · 2/2"; One Piece "Leader · OP15-058 · 5000 Power · 5 Life", which wraps in a narrow column; data, so the dash and the dots stay). Raised with `HERO_CARD_SHADOW`'s values from `md` (`md:shadow-lg md:shadow-primary/25 md:dark:shadow-black/60`, written out because the constant can't take a variant), and this card's lift and ring moved onto U2's tokens, 120 ms on settle-120 (CardImage's own 150 ms on the CSS default curve stays everywhere else, LATER row 230). A real hover measured `translate: 0px -2px`. Reduced motion: no lift (`motion-safe:`), the ring at once. Phones keep today's card (160 × 286.54 at 390 with touch, the mono caption, the untinted `shadow-md`) until U4a: the facts line is hidden below `md` and the caption from it. The share page passes no `raised` (unchanged on prod).
- **The Skeleton is the same row**, changed in the same edit: the card's box at the raised width, a name bar beside it and two no-break-space lines (the phone caption's and the facts line's). Measured with the precon fetch gated in the page: at 1440 the section 304.81 px, the row 776 × 250.81, the card 180 × 250.81, the facts column 580 × 44 (the name row 24 px at y 213, the facts line 16 px at y 237), identical before and after the seed; at 390, 160 × 286.54 before and after. The raised leader section is 15.7 px shorter than U3a's stacked card at `md` and up (250.81 against 266.5): a layout change, not a shift.
- **The header's name and chip.** The name keeps `min-w-[8rem]` (128 px) where it had `min-w-0`; the chip is `min-w-0 shrink` with its words in a truncating span, hidden from `md` to `wide`. Because the name input is `flex: 1 1 auto` at `lg` (its `lg:basis-auto`), the chip shrinks with the name rather than strictly first: squeezing the header at `wide` (its width forced, the viewport at 1200), the name measured 204 / 152 / 132 / 128 / 128 px and the chip 193 / 144 / 125 / 89 / 49 px at a 900 / 800 / 760 / 720 / 680 px header, with no overflow. With real content the name is 487 px at 1200 and 451 px at 1024, and 219 px at 1024 beside a 224 px fork credit (with U3a's visible 210 px chip that forked name would have been about 1 px). Phones keep the chip until U4a: hiding it there would have reflowed the phone header's three rows. **LATER row 229 closed**: at 768 the header is two rows (87 px), the cluster on the first beside the mark.
- **CLS unchanged** (layout-shift entries, U3a's throttle: 4x CPU, Fast 4G, dark): dev 1440 0.0020 before and after, 1200 0.0029, 390 0.0074 before and after; prod after the deploy 1440 0.0020 and 390 0.0074, the same movers as U3a's (the Tools pane's attribution and the search keycaps at 1440, the phone header's cluster at 390).
- **Found in passing**: CardImage's raw `transition` shorthand, which U2's acceptance grep couldn't see (LATER row 230); U1's edge cast (row 231); under `prefers-contrast: more` the hairlines that come back are still the 1.28 to 1.47:1 panel line (row 233). LATER row 215 (an always-on gradient, only if U3b shipped without the gate) closed unfired.

### U4a decisions and deviations from `WAVE5.md` D5 (2026-10-14, `cdf0b5f`)

D5's first four paragraphs shipped in one session: the phone header in fixed rows, one count, the leader card beside its facts, one controls row with the "Group · Sort" popover, 44 px on touch. No migration, no route (the `pnpm build` route table byte-identical), no dependency. What the package decided:

- **The status row is every deck's, not only a draft's (deviation, the owner's call of 2026-10-14).** D5 gives a saved deck one row (the mark, the name, More), but §2's header contract keeps the save status in a fixed slot with "Save failed" and Retry outside any menu, and Share as a saved deck's primary action; at 360 px the 144 px save slot cannot sit beside a name at its 8rem floor (mark 44 + name 128 + slot 144 + More 44 and the gaps is 382 px of a 336 px row). And a row that exists only while a draft vanishes when the first edit mints the deck: the pane would jump up 40 px about a second after that edit. Asked with both layouts, the owner chose "Two fixed rows for every deck". Below `md` the header is a grid, never a wrap (`grid-cols-[auto_minmax(0,1fr)_auto]`, rows 56 px, auto, 40 px): row 1 the mark, the name (its 8rem floor kept, 44 px on touch, an ellipsis when it overflows) and More; a forked deck's credit line under the name (the auto row, empty for every other deck); the status row with the save slot under the name's text (x 72 at 390, its label left-aligned, the outgoing cross-fade label anchored to the same left edge) and the primary action at the right (Keep this deck on a seeded draft, Share once a row exists, nothing on an unseeded draft). The status row is 44 px on touch: D5's `h-10` cannot hold a 44 px Keep, and D5 already says touch wins for the controls row. Measured: 101 px of chrome on a touch phone (97 on a fine pointer) for every deck, against 149 before and D5's hoped 57 for a saved deck; nothing appears, disappears or moves when a draft is kept or minted.
- **The chip steps aside below `wide` (deviation from "the format chip rides beside the name as 12 px meta").** Its words are 192 px at 12 px; the name's track is 262 px at 390 and 232 at 360, so beside it the name field would get 62 / 32 px, or, holding the name's floor, the chip 126 / 96 px with "Calling All Angels" (134.5 px of text in a 16 px-padded field) cut to 128 px as well. The comp has no chip at all. It hides from `md` to `wide` already (U3b), so it is now a wide-only element.
- **Appearance in More, an inline group.** On phones More ends with a separator and a group labelled "Appearance": the three theme radios (each closes the menu, as in the Appearance menu) and the Background art switch (keeps it open). Not a submenu: one tap instead of two, and a side submenu at 390 px has no room to open beside More. The items are `AppearanceItems`, shared with `AppearanceMenu` (its hooks now live in the items, which only render while a menu is open). The header's own control renders only from `md`: `phone` (the editor's tier) gates the group and the control, the R4 rule (CSS decides widths, JavaScript which control exists), because a CSS-hidden copy would sit in Base UI's item list and in jsdom. One appearance control per page at every tier, pinned in jsdom with `phone` true and false and in the editor's tier-flip test. The draft More list pin holds exactly (the group's items are `menuitemradio` and `menuitemcheckbox`, not `menuitem`); the phone's group is pinned beside it. Driven in Brave at 390 with real clicks: Light switched the theme and closed More; Background art wrote its preference and kept More open.
- **Found: on dev the editor's first paint is the tier's server snapshot.** `next dev` server-renders `/decks/new` (the dev HTML carries the editor with `data-tier="wide"`; prod's static shell carries only the `BAILOUT_TO_CLIENT_SIDE_RENDERING` marker and renders the editor client-side with the real tier). With the header's control gated by the tier alone, a dev phone painted it for about 320 ms, auto-placed into the empty fork row: a 145 px header, then 101, a 0.0465 shift. The control now sits in a `contents max-md:hidden` wrapper, so it never takes a phone's row, whatever the tier says. Prod's first observed commit was already `phone`. `use-tier.ts`'s docblock said the snapshot never paints; it now says it doesn't on prod and does on dev.
- **One count.** Below `md` the pane's "Deck" heading is `sr-only` (the tab says "Deck · N"); the ring leads its count at the left (`order-first`), the count in its own span at 14 / 500 in the foreground with the words muted ("100 / 100" then "cards", or "98 / 100" then "· 2 to go"), "Add cards" at the row's right (its group `order-last`, still `self-stretch`). From `md` the label stays all muted, U3a's form; its text is unchanged, so two `getByText` pins in `deck-editor.test.tsx` ("0 / 100 · 100 to go" at 375 and "0 / 50 · 50 to go" at 1440) moved to the slot's `textContent`, the words the same. The over-limit link is 44 px on a phone's touch. With a collection imported the ownership line keeps its place before the count, so the ring and the count part (LATER row 238).
- **The leader card: 160 px with the 45 % cap, its facts beside it, at every tier.** The raised row (U3b) now runs on phones: the card keeps today's phone box (160 × 222.95 at 390, 151.2 × 210.7 at 360, 45 % of the pane at most, rather than D5's pure 45 %, 164.7 px at 390), so the LCP image's box is unchanged; beside it the name at 14 / 500 (truncating), Remove (44 px on touch) and the facts line (`leaderFacts`, `.type-meta` muted, the caption inside it at every tier, so the mono caption is the share page's alone). On a phone's narrow column the facts wrap `pretty` (`max-md:text-pretty`): at 360 "Legendary Creature — Angel" stays whole above "· 2/2" instead of "Legendary Creature — Angel ·" over "2/2" (measured; `balance` split the type line). The hero shadow (the primary at 25 % on ivory, black at 60 % on charcoal) is now the phone's too. The leader section is 276.9 px at 390 (340.5 before). The Skeleton keeps step: with the precon fetch gated, the row 366 × 222.95 at 390 and 336 × 210.68 at 360, the card box and the facts column identical before and after the seed (at 360 the facts line wraps to two lines inside the column, 64 → 80 px, the row the card's height either way).
- **The "Group · Sort" trigger.** An outline `sm` button with a chevron (`data-icon="inline-end"`), at the row's right beside View; the label is the new copy (one middle dot) and shows nothing of the current choice (the headings say the grouping and the rows the order, and a value in the label would not fit beside View at 360). The popover (`ui/popover.tsx`, Base UI, its first use) opens below, aligned to the trigger's end; its grid lines the two labels in one column with the two groups beside them (`*:contents` on the Segmenteds' wrappers). Focus: Base UI's defaults, the first tabbable inside (the pressed Group item) or the popup itself on touch, and back to the trigger on close: the trigger never unmounts, so Y7b's `finalFocus` trap does not apply; jsdom pins Escape and the return with focus inside first, and a mutation (`finalFocus={false}`) fails it. A pick leaves the popover open for the other group. Measured in Brave at 390: the popover 216.8 × 124, its items 44 × 44 on touch; Escape closed it and focus was on the trigger, on dev and on prod. Under reduced motion (U2's CSSOM-copy method) its `dw-in` runs 0.01 ms. From `md` the inline row as U3a left it.
- **44 px on touch, and the counting rule.** A target is every activatable element (buttons, links with an `href`, inputs, the button, tab, menu item, checkbox, radio, switch and link roles, anything with a `tabindex` of 0 or more; not tab panels, groups, meters, `main` or sections) that is rendered (visible with CSS, not inside `[hidden]` or `[inert]`, larger than 1 × 1 px) and intersects the first screen (above the tab bar's top, the bar itself included), counted by its own border box; it is under 44 when either side is (0.01 px tolerance), and a row's name button counts by its own box because the row has no handler. Menus closed. Before: 2 at 390 (the name input 366 × 32, the first row's name button 94 × 20) and 1 at 360 (the name input; the first row sat under the tab bar). After: 0 at 390 (30 targets) and 360 (26), both shapes and themes. The name input is 44 px on a phone's touch; an editable row's name button stretches to its row on any coarse pointer (`pointer-coarse:self-stretch`; a fine pointer changes nothing and the share page's rows keep theirs). Menu items stay 28 px (LATER row 235); a fork's credit link is a 16 px line (row 236).
- **Measured** (Brave through the DevTools MCP, the Giada precon draft and a saved deck's shape through a throwaway local page that answered the deck read from the precon's public wire and swallowed every write, deleted with `.next/dev` before the check and the build; zero writes). 390 with touch: the header 101 px in both shapes, the deck pane from y 101, the first card row at y 600.9 (764.5 before), the controls one 46 px row (two before); 360: the same rows, no horizontal overflow (the draft's layout was 374 px wide before and the page zoomed out), the first row at 588.7. CLS (U3a's throttle, dark): dev 390 0.0074 → 0.0001, 360 0.0001 (before, the overflow's zoom-out flagged every entry as input: 0 by the metric, 0.0155 raw), 1440 0.0021 before and after; prod after the deploy 390 0.0001 and 1440 0.0021 (U3b's 0.0074 and 0.0020), the 1440 movers U3a's. Every box of the header, the deck pane and the tab bar at 768, 1024, 1200 and 1440 (the draft) and 768 and 1440 (a saved deck) is identical before and after, the count's inner span the one DOM difference (the same box, 14 px, 400, muted); the leader image's shadow at 768 unchanged. Contrast: no new pair (the count in the foreground is the bracket lead's 16.66 / 12.9:1, "cards" and the facts line muted on the page 8.57 / 6.04:1, "Draft" and "Saved" over the chrome U3b's worst case 6.23 / 4.87:1, the trigger U1's outline). Prod, signed out, zero creates (five GETs on the editor): the two rows, Appearance inside More, one count, the leader row, the one controls row and its popover at 390 and 360; 1440 unchanged; the share page unchanged (`/d/jhr5ax43ewx7`: the stacked card with its mono caption, the ruled headings, no Group · Sort).
- **Pins moved, said here**: `editor-header.test.tsx`'s primary slot (U3a's `max-md:contents` → the status row's right end); U3b's raised leader tests (the `md:` classes → every tier, the mono caption out of the raised row, the hero shadow without `md:`); the two `getByText` count pins above. Held: "Deck · 34", U3a's View / Group / Sort pin (the `md` form), the draft More list, every `aria-label`. Five mutation checks (the popover's focus return, the CSS guard, the group at every tier, the stretch, the tier wiring), all caught.
- **Left for later**: the menu primitive's 28 px items on touch (LATER row 235), a fork's credit link (236), the phone header's focus order (237: name, then Keep or Share, then More, because the DOM keeps the `md` order), the ownership line between the ring and the count (238), "Keyboard shortcuts ?" in a phone's More (239). LATER row 228 closed.

### U4b decisions and deviations from `WAVE5.md` D5 (2026-10-14, `36340df`)

D5's last two paragraphs shipped in one session, so there is no U4c: the phone card sheet on three snap points, non-modal at the peek and the half, the re-target in place, the release split by `data-swiping`, and one Tools strip on phones. No migration, no route (the `pnpm build` route table byte-identical), no dependency. What the package decided:

- **`drawer.tsx` gains three opt-in props rather than the comp's composition from its parts.** `DrawerContent` takes `overlay` (`"modal"`, the default, or `"always"`), `overlayClassName` and `flickRelease`. Composing like the comp would have copied the popup's long class strings into the editor, and the comp's copy had already drifted from the wrapper (450 ms and a raw curve). The `md` tools drawer, the Swap, Swap in, Fit, Games and bracket sheets and the card page's printings drawer pass none of them and render as before: pinned (the `md` drawer has no snap points, no scrim and no release class) and measured (its 56 boxes identical at 768 and 1024, still non-modal with no scrim).
- **The scrim renders at every point, not only at the full one (a deviation from the comp, which mounted it at the full point).** It is 40 % black (`oklab(0 0 0 / 0.4)`) with Base UI's minimum overlay opacity set to 0, so Base UI's own snap progress (1 at the half and below) holds it at opacity 0 below full, fades it under the finger on a drag between the half and the full point, and fades it out on a release rather than unmounting it. The comp's way unmounts a scrim that a fast flick away from the full point leaves about a third visible, a pop. Below full it takes no pointer events, and it has no backdrop blur: the primitive's 4 px blur would have switched on as a step when the snap lands.
- **What each point shows: one pane, laid out per point.** `CardDetailPane` takes `sheet` (the point, the frame's Close, the actions) and renders a fixed header row (the name; then the type line, the stats and the cost on one muted line; Close at the end) over a column that renders by point: the actions at the peek, the card from the half, and at the full point everything the pane shows elsewhere (the text, tags, the footer line, Alternatives, Printings). The card is the smaller of 60 % of the column and the height the half leaves under the header and the actions, at the card's ratio (214.8 × 299.3 px at 390, 196.8 × 274.2 at 360), so it fits the half on short phones too. Rendering by point, not clipping, keeps the column shorter than the sheet below full, so a drag up on the card moves the sheet; Base UI hands a touch to a scrollable column first, which would have scrolled it instead. The pane's own layout (the Card tab, the `md` drawer, the wide pane) is unchanged, box for box.
- **Close at every point (a deviation from the comp, which showed it only at the full point).** A non-modal sheet at the peek still needs a visible, focusable way out for a screen-reader or switch user; Escape covers keyboards. 44 × 44 on touch at every point.
- **The actions are the editor's own paths, each where it applies.** Add for a card the deck doesn't hold (the search pane's add with its toast and Undo; "Swap in…" at the deck's maximum, Y7b), Remove for one it does (the row's remove and its Undo; the leader's too), Swap… where Swap Lab offers alternatives (a main-list non-land in Magic, never in One Piece). The comp showed all three on every card (a fixture); a card the deck already holds gets no Add, because a second copy is the row's stepper and the copy rule lives inside the adapter's `validate` (LATER row 243). Swap… and Swap in… close the card sheet first, so neither sheet opens over a card that is about to leave; focus comes back as from a row (Y7b). The sheet's footer links are 44 px on touch; the pane's text links elsewhere are unchanged (LATER row 245).
- **The re-target.** The body is keyed on the card's id inside the same popup; a key change while the sheet stays open marks the new body `data-swapped`, which runs `dw-in` (250 ms on settle-250) only `motion-safe:`; the first body after an open never fades. At the full point the sheet is modal and pointer dismissal is on, so a tap on the scrim closes it rather than re-targeting. Measured at 390: the same popup node and box (0, 379.8, 390 × 748) before and after a re-target, the new body's opacity 0 to 0.92 in about 180 ms.
- **The release split lives in `drawer.tsx`** (`useFlickRelease`): a capture-phase pointer-up (a mouse or a pen) or touch-end / touch-cancel (Base UI releases touch through touch events) marks the popup `data-released="swipe"` when it is `data-swiping` with a non-zero inline swipe movement, before Base UI's own handler drops `data-swiping`; the next press clears it, and so does a 450 ms timer. The mark switches the popup to flick-400 over 400 ms × `--drawer-swipe-strength`. A press without movement (Close, the handle) carries no mark and keeps settle-400, as do a tap-open and Escape.
