Driven in Brave with scripted mouse drags on the handle (the DevTools MCP has no touch drag): half to full ran 400 ms on flick-400 (strength 1); a fast flick from the peek closed on flick-400 × 0.1 (40 ms for its last 19 px); Escape at the full point closed on settle-400 with no mark. jsdom drives Base UI's own swipe with stubbed heights and `elementFromPoint`, so the points, the modal flip and the mark are pinned end to end.
- **Reduced motion.** U2's exception already makes the drawer's popup opacity-only at 150 ms, so a snap lands with no travel (measured through U2's CSSOM-copy method: at the full point's top within two frames of the release) and the release mark's curve is overridden; the swap's fade is `motion-safe:` only, so the body swaps at once.
- **The strip is the phone's alone.** D5 sits in the phone section and the lab's finding was three navigations in one scroll at 390. From `md` the analytics and the hand sit under the deck list beside what they describe (U3a and U3b measured that pane), and the `md` drawer and the wide pane keep the four tabs. On phones: Card · Suggestions · Combos · Cuts · Analytics · Hand (One Piece: Card · Analytics · Hand). The analytics render as the bare `AnalyticsBlocks` in their panel; the hand is `SampleHand bare`: no collapsible and no heading (the tab names it), its Draw button first, and "Add cards to draw a sample hand." while the deck is empty (new copy). The labels are 13 px and each tab grows from its own width; a plain equal-width share with the 44 px touch minimum made all six 63.7 px and cut "Suggestions". At 360 the six take the 352 px row exactly, every tab at least 44 × 47 on touch; a wider strip scrolls sideways rather than wrap or clip (LATER row 244). A dealt card opens the sheet without flipping the strip to Card, so the hand is still there when the sheet closes; every other inspection still lands on the Card tab. A strip that loses its tab across a tier flip shows Card.
- **Measured** (Brave through the DevTools MCP, the Giada precon draft, state-only; zero writes). At 390 with touch: the peek 144 px (17.1 %), the half 464.2 (55 %), the full point 748 (88.6 %, the popup's 100dvh − 6rem) with the scrim at opacity 1 in `oklab(0 0 0 / 0.4)`; at 360: 144, 429, 684. Below full: the viewport non-modal, the scrim at opacity 0 and click-through. Targets under 44 px by U4a's rule, inside the sheet: 0 at every point at 390 and 360 (3 at the peek and the half, 10 at the full point); before, 0 of 7 in today's single modal sheet. No transition over 400 ms. The Deck tab at 390 and 360 with the sheet closed identical box for box (1,382 boxes each); the `md` drawer at 768 and 1024 identical; every box at 1024, 1200 and 1440 identical, and at 768 all but the bracket line, which the before capture caught mid-read. CLS with U3a's throttle: 390 0.0001, 360 0.0001, 1440 0.0020 (U4a's 0.0001, 0.0001, 0.0021; the same movers). Both themes. Prod after the deploy, signed out, five GETs and no write: a row tap opened the half, a tap on the leader card behind it re-targeted the same popup, a drag reached the full point with the 40 % scrim and the flick mark, Escape closed it; the strip at 390 the same six widths; 1440 identical to dev's before capture; the share page's sample hand unchanged.
- **Pins moved, said here.** `deck-editor.test.tsx`'s tier test: the sheet opens at the half, so "Card page" (the full point's) left it; its two headings and the Add action are pinned instead. `editor-layout.test.tsx`'s sheet test: non-modal at the half with the scrim click-through (focus still moves into the sheet on open). Held: "Deck · 34", "Suggestions", "Cuts", "Sample hand" (the `md` extras and the share page), "Draw sample hand", every `aria-label`, and the zero-write pins (opening, re-targeting, snapping and closing write nothing). Eight mutation checks (the modal flip, the scrim at every point, the body's key, the mark's movement test, the capture handler, the Hand tab's in-place inspection, Add only for a card the deck doesn't hold, the half's image), all caught.
- **The owner's real-phone checks (D5's two, plus LATER row 232), on prod after the deploy**: a re-grab during a release continues from the sheet's on-screen position, no jump, so the release stays at 400 ms and nothing goes upstream; at the full point the scrim fades in and a tap above the sheet closes it, and at the half a tap on another deck row re-targets the sheet in place; the phone tab bar's blur stays smooth under a fast scroll, so it keeps the material (row 232 closed).
- **Left for later**: the other drawers' release split (LATER row 240), focus at the modal flip (241), the printings refetch after a re-target (242), copies from the sheet (243), the strip's last pixel at 360 (244), a tablet's footer links (245). Row 232 closed.

### U7a decisions and deviations from `WAVE5.md` D8 (2026-10-15, `16d4159`)

D8's first three paragraphs shipped in one session: the image grid by default on both indexes, "Build a deck" on `/leaders`, the 96 px full-card tiles on a two-column grid. No migration, no route (the `pnpm build` route table byte-identical), no dependency. What the package decided:

- **The default flips in the lib, one constant.** `DEFAULT_INDEX_VIEW` is `"grid"` (`src/lib/hub/index-view.ts`), so an empty, missing or corrupt stored value reads as the grid (D8's corrupt-value test now renders the grid) and the store's server snapshot, the server HTML of both `ƒ` routes, is the grid. A stored `"list"` renders the list after hydration; the toggle, the key, the pick banner and `?q=` are untouched. The grid's server HTML keeps what the list gave crawlers and the smokes: every `/c/` or `/l/` link in the same order (diffed against the before capture: 60 on `/commanders`, 142 on `/leaders`), each with the rank or the printed number, the name and the identity as text. `smoke:hubs` passes unchanged, its first-`href` ranking check included.
- **The swap is keyed (beyond D8).** Both views' lists sit in one conditional, so React reused the rows and moved them when hydration swapped views: today's Grid reader paid a layout shift of 0.261 on `/leaders` and 0.2521 on `/commanders` at 1440. With the grid as the server HTML that cost would have passed to List readers; each list now carries the view as its key, so the swap replaces the rows. Measured: a stored list at 1440 and 390 shows no layout-shift entry, on dev and on prod (0.2521 with the keys removed, a mutation the new RTL case catches).
- **The first six cards are eager (the lazy-loading decision, measured).** CardImage's lazy default renders at opacity 0 until hydration, so with every card lazy the LCP waited for hydration and the fade: 4,364 ms at 390 with 4x CPU and Fast 4G on a cold cache, against 1,264 ms with the first six on `priority` (eager, `fetchpriority="high"`, React's image preload, no fade), FCP the same (1,156 against 1,132). Six is 1440's first row (six columns of the 8.5rem grid in the 992 px column) and three rows at 390. The rest stay lazy (LATER row 246 for the cards past six). Weight at load, cold cache: `/leaders` 12 images and 116,648 B at 390, 42 and 406,954 B at 1440 (the list loaded none; all 142 are 1,381,394 B, P4.9's 1.4 MB); `/commanders` 12 and 164,693 B, 42 and 590,435 B (all 60, 843,980 B).
- **"Build a deck"** on `/leaders` (unpinned; the home card's words): the header row unchanged (992 × 36 at 1440, 358 × 92 at 390), the button 106.6 px wide where "Start a deck" was 106.2.
- **The tile's art box is a 96 × 134.1 px full card** at the small rendition's ratio, so `object-cover` crops nothing and the artist and © line stays visible; the gradient fallback fills the same box. The tile grows from 108.2 to 164.1 px tall. The comp's B tile also took more padding, wider gaps and a 16 px name; D8 keeps the name and the meta as they are, so the padding, the gaps and the 14 px name stay (U5 may revisit home's).
- **The name runs to two lines (a deviation from "the name unchanged": its words, size and link are unchanged, its overflow isn't).** In the two-column boxes 640 or 672 px wide (`/u`, `/f`, `/account`, the hub's shelves) the 96 px card leaves a 180 to 196 px text column, where a one-line name cut 5 of 14 on `/u` (1 of 14 before). A two-line clamp that breaks long words uses the room the card's height leaves: measured everywhere from 360 to 1440, no name clipped and no text column taller than the card, so the tile is the card's height; on `/u` at 1440 nine names sit on one line and five on two.
- **The grid: one column below `md`, two from it** (was two from `sm` and three from `lg`), the comp's B grid. Two columns from `md` keep the text column at a phone's width or more (228 px at 768 in home's container, 356 at 1024 and 1440; two columns from `sm` would have left 164 at 640). Home's three sections follow the base; the five sites' `lg:grid-cols-2` overrides (D8 assumed three columns there; they were already two) went as redundant, the hub's shelves keeping their `mt-2`, and `BrowserDeckTiles`' `className` prop went with its last caller. `/precons` keeps its own class: three columns from `xl` (1280) and two at 1024 and 768 as before, but one column from 640 to 767 now (two before), and the taller tile makes it longer at 390, 23,151 → 33,267 px (33,123 at 700): U7b's fold (D8: under 6,000 px) owns that page's length, and its `lg:grid-cols-2` is now redundant too.
- **Measured** (Brave through the DevTools MCP, dev, both themes; the pages wrote nothing). The default grid on both indexes identical box for box to today's stored grid at 1440 and 390 (every region; the one difference the button's label); a stored list identical to today's list; a junk value renders the grid. Everything outside the tile grids identical box for box on home (the hero, the Magic region, the capabilities, the outro, shifted down by the taller rail: 670.7 px at 390, 575.8 at 1440), the hub (every section but the two shelves, at 390 and 1440), `/u` and `/f`. Tiles: 358 × 164.1 at 390 (a 224 px text column), 328 × 164.1 at 360 (194; three of home's names on two lines), 608 × 164.1 at 640, 362 × 164.1 at 768, 490 × 164.1 at 1024 and 1440; one link per tile, ♥ where a like exists, no horizontal overflow at 360. The signed-in account tiles and the guest list through a throwaway local page of fixtures (deleted with `.next/dev` before the check and the build): their actions inside the tile (the column ending 145 to 147 px down the 164.1 px tile at 390), the ⋯ trigger 44 × 44 on touch, the "Share page" link 63 × 16 (LATER row 248). CLS 0 on both indexes and home at 390 and 1440, also under 4x CPU and Fast 4G (the LCP there: `/leaders` 972 and 940 ms on a card, `/commanders` 700 on a card at 390 and 656 on the intro at 1440, home 1,280 and 1,268 on the H1). `smoke:hubs`, `smoke:seo` and `smoke:engagement` green on dev (the smokes deleted their fixture decks and users; the census unchanged).

### U5 decisions and deviations from `WAVE5.md` D6 (2026-10-15, `8e80f85`, fix `ec3caf8`)

D6's home B shipped in one session as one code commit and one follow-up fix found on prod the same hour. No migration, no route (the `pnpm build` route table byte-identical after each), no dependency; home stays `ƒ`. What the package decided:

- **The fan's rows: one statement, nine rows, split in rank order.** `loadTopCommanders(FAN_SIZE + SHELF_SIZE)` (its query already filters `slug IS NOT NULL`, so nine rows are nine hubs) and `magicHome()` in `src/lib/home/shelves.ts`: the fan takes the first three as `normal` renditions (`embeddablePrintingImageUrl(…, "normal")`, 488 × 680, an override served as stored), the shelf the next six as `small`, a short corpus shortening the shelf first. The no-reuse check over the served HTML (dev and prod): fan 3, Magic shelf 6, One Piece shelf 6, rail 12, no printing on two sections; the one repeat is inside the rail (two Goreclaw decks, D0's exception). The most played sits in the center: the DOM order is rank order (the center first, on top), so a screen reader hears 1, 2, 3 while the eye reads 2, 1, 3. A card with no image is CardImage's named box, and the rule targets children, not `img`.
- **Amended 2026-10-17 (the owner's call): the fan rotates daily over the index's top 30.** The owner asked whether the fan's images could change daily from the most popular commanders: the rank refreshes nightly (the 10:37 UTC ingest) but the top three almost never moves, so the fan looked frozen. Now `loadTopCommanders(POOL_SIZE)` (30; the LIMIT 9 → 30, still one statement — three for a guest's GET, counted with DB_LOG on dev) and `magicHome(rows, day)` in `src/lib/home/shelves.ts`: the pool is the first 30 slugged rows (the query's order, most played first; rows past the pool are ignored), the fan a seeded partial Fisher–Yates draw of three from it (mulberry32, W9a's generator, seeded by FNV-1a over the UTC date `YYYY-MM-DD`; the page reads the date once per request with `utcDayKey(new Date())` and the split never reads the clock, so the same rows and day always give the same fan), listed in rank order so the day's highest-ranked is the first element — the center card, on top (`.home-fan > :first-child`), a screen reader still hearing the most played first; the shelf the six highest-ranked of the rest in rank order, so the most-played commanders are always on the page (in the fan on their day, on the shelf otherwise) and D0's no-reuse rule holds by construction. C(30, 3) = 4,060 fans; the shelf changes on the days the fan takes one of the top six (about half of them). A short corpus still fills the fan first (three of five rows fan, two shelve). Nothing rendered claims a top three: the fan has no caption and the shelf's clause ("Ranked by EDHREC decklist count via Scryfall, not games played") stays true. The CSS, the geometry and the LCP decision are untouched; no migration, no route, no dependency. Measured on dev in the pane (1024 × 768, signed out): the live home on 2026-10-18 UTC fanned Etali, Primal Storm (rank 2, the center), Kutzil, Malamet Exemplar (8) and Niv-Mizzet, Parun (29) with the shelf ranks 1, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7 (Syr Konrad, Ragavan, Braids, Azusa, Loran, Toski); a forced 2026-10-17 (a throwaway page, never staged) fanned Ragavan (3), Sheoldred, Whispering One (25) and Veyran (27) with the shelf ranks 1, 2, 4, 5, 6, 7; the pool is `/commanders`' first 30 hubs, slug for slug; no printing on both the fan and the shelf, 27 images, three eager, the one repeat the rail's commander thumbnail (D0's exception; Niv-Mizzet that day); the artist and © strips 0 of 144 sampled points covered on each card (`elementFromPoint` through each card's translate and rotate; the center 201 × 280, the sides 238 × 305; a hidden pane reads zero-size boxes, so paint it with a screenshot first); three statements for the GET (the Magic rows, the finishes, the rail), as before. D6's section 1 and the decisions table carry the same note; LATER row 249 annotated (a returning visitor's warm-cache case is rarer; the cold numbers unchanged). Pins: `shelves.test.ts` (the sizes 3 / 6 / 30; the same day and rows → the same fan and shelf; another day → other cards, a week not one fan; the fan drawn from the pool in rank order with the highest-ranked first; the shelf the six highest-ranked of the rest, no card in both; the renditions; rows past the pool never fan or shelve nor change the draw; an override and a missing printing; a short corpus; `utcDayKey` the UTC date whatever the zone), `page.test.tsx` (thirty rows asked for; the fan is `magicHome`'s for the UTC date pinned with a fake `Date`, the shelf the six highest-ranked of the rest; the next date fans other cards and the same date renders the same fan), `hero-fan.test.tsx` (array order, the first the center). Mutation checks, each red: the shelf ignoring the fan (4 tests), a constant seed (2), a clock in the seed (4), the fan reversed (4), the pool cap dropped (1), the page passing a fixed day (2), the page asking for nine rows (3).
- **The fan's geometry: the side cards sit lower (a deviation from the comp, for D6's own rule).** In the comp's geometry the upright center card covered 56 of 147 sampled points of each turned card's bottom strip (92 to 98 % of the height, 3 to 97 % of the width; `elementFromPoint` through each card's own translate and rotate): the left card's copyright side and the right card's artist side. D6 says every artist and © line is uncovered and D0 says never covered, so the side cards drop by 14 % of a card height (`--fan-drop`): 0.08 left 52 of 294 points covered, 0.10 left 24, 0.12 left 4, 0.13 none; 0.14 keeps a step of margin. The fan reads as a slight arch. Measured 0 covered at 1440, 390 and 360 on dev and at 390 on prod. Sizes: 280 px from `md`, 180 below it, clamped to `(100vw − 1rem) / 1.88` so a screen under 360 px never scrolls sideways (the fan is 1.88 card heights wide with the turns); the box is `1.05 × h + drop + 2.5rem` tall, explicit, so nothing shifts when the cards land.
- **The LCP decision: all three cards `priority` (the comp's, measured; D6 named the center).** A turned card's bounding box is larger than the upright one's (238 × 305 against 201 × 280 at 280 px), so whichever card paints last is the LCP, whatever the priority. With the center alone on `priority` the side cards were lazy, so they stayed at opacity 0 until hydration and the fade: the LCP was a side card at 4,504 ms (dev, 1440, 4x CPU, Fast 4G, cold). With all three eager they paint within ~40 ms of each other (2,264 ms on the same run). On prod, cold: 1440 FCP 1,252 and 1,076 ms, LCP 1,924 and 2,036 ms on a fan card (before U5: 1,372 on the H1); warm 720 ms. **D6's "under 1.0 s" is met on a warm cache only**: the three JPEGs (270,588 B) share the throttled link with the fonts (162,692 B); LATER row 249 holds the levers. At 390 the H1 is the larger box and stays the LCP (prod cold 1,068 and 1,884 ms against 1,296 before).
- **The H1's width pinned so both faces wrap alike (the fix, `ec3caf8`).** On prod after `8e80f85`, at 390 cold, the fan's three high-priority preloads delayed Literata past first paint and the H1 reflowed from the fallback's two lines to three, a 50 px shift under the sentence and the fan (0.0561; the touch emulation marks it as input, so only the raw entries show it). The faces disagree in boxes 352 to 382 px (3 lines against 2: phones of 384 to 414 px) and 700 to 750 (2 against 1: 768 to 782), so the H1 is 7em below `md` (336 px, three lines in both), 12em from `md` (two in both) and its column from `lg` (one; xl's third holds two in both): every width from 360 to 1279 measured the same count in both faces. On prod after the fix, two cold runs at 390 recorded no layout-shift entry. The same mismatch may exist for other serif titles (LATER row 251).
- **The hero's split from `xl` only, a third and two-thirds; stacked below.** At `lg` halves the 280 px fan (526 px wide with its turns) overflows a 476 px column and, at 1024, the viewport by about 9 px. Stacked at 1024 to 1279 the H1 is one line and the fan sits centered under the words. Gate 5 at 1280 × 800 in both themes: the H1 two lines in a 402.7 px column, the sentence 15 words, "Build a deck" (an `lg` primary to `/decks/new`) ending at 437.6 px, `pt-16`, three text elements (the H1, the sentence, the button row); before U5 the first "Build a deck" ended at 496 px inside the Magic card. The second action is the ghost "Browse commanders" (F's default).
- **Headings: D6's ladder, not the comp's sizes.** The game, rail and capability h2s are `.type-section` (Literata 18 / 26, the comp drew 24 px); the shelf headings are serif h3s on `.type-small` (14 / 20), the next step down, so h2 and h3 differ; the H1 stays `.type-display` (48 / 50 at every width: a second `.type-*` rule fails `tokens.test.ts`, so no 36 px phone step). Zero uppercase section labels on home (four before: "Open beta", the two shelf labels, "Recent public decks"); the One Piece cards' printed ids keep their badge.
- **Region names unchanged.** The game sections keep their `aria-label`s ("Magic: The Gathering · Commander", "One Piece Card Game"), the shelves theirs, the rail and "What Deckwarden does" theirs (now beside a visible h2 of the same words); `id="your-decks"` and `<main id="main">` unchanged.
- **The One Piece field: 8 % in both themes, a quarter and three quarters from `xl`.** The accent mixed into the page in oklab (U3b's lesson on oklch casts), `rounded-2xl`, the words and the stacked buttons left. D6's thirds made the six cards 118 px and clipped "Donquixote Rosinante"; a quarter gives 134 px with nothing clipped and the blurb on two lines (the comp's 1 : 3.4 gave 138 px and three). Contrast in the field: lowest 7.50 on dark and 5.32 on light (the comp measured 7.58 / 5.53 with 6 % on light), so D6's 8 % holds on both.
- **The Magic region: one default and three ghosts** (Browse commanders was outline), every href unchanged, each label the `<a>`'s whole content (the Surprise me regex); the six cards three by two below `lg` and one row from it at up to 152 px; the clause "Ranked by EDHREC decklist count via Scryfall, not games played." The shelf cards keep CardImage's `frame` (the ring and the lift on the image itself, now animated; the comp lifted the whole link).
- **The motion: CardImage fixed site-wide, the tile lift site-wide (measured everywhere).** CardImage's list names `translate` on the tokens (LATER row 230 closed): every framed card's 2 px lift animates for the first time, on home, the index grids, `/cards`, the editor and the share page; the editor's raised leader card dropped U3b's override, which named `transform` too. `DeckTile` lifts 2 px on hover over 120 ms on settle-120 (`background-color, translate`) on every collection rather than home alone, so one tile behaves one way: measured at rest `translate: none` on every tile on home (12), the Atraxa hub (3), `/u/bobandis6` (14), `/f/veunykz3yuje` (13), `/precons` (181) and the signed-out `/account` (2 fixture tiles), the boxes U7a's (164.1 px tall), and the lift under hover on home and the hub; a `transitionrun` for `translate` recorded on a tile and a shelf card. Reduced motion (the emulator's switch): no lift, the tint and the ring still change.
- **The rails: U7a's tiles as they are** (D6's words; the comp's roomier tile not taken): two columns from `md` at 618 × 164.1 px in the wide container at 1440. D6's two meta lines ("Magic · Commander" / "Updated … by …") are the tile's dot policy, U9's (D9: the tiles drop the dot before "Updated"). Continue building sits in a wrapper that is `empty:hidden`, so a guest without decks gets no hairline and the server HTML no words (`smoke:engagement`); the guest's section measured on the real home through a claim-token and fetch shim (no request left the page, no row minted), the account's through the page test.
- **What Deckwarden does: an offset list.** The serif h2 left and a description list (`dt` / `dd`) in the right half under a gold rule, D6's titles over R5a's bodies (their em dashes are U9's).
- **The outro and LATER row 166.** The crest at 32 px and one serif sentence with the sign-in link. The Moxfield sentence left home; the chooser's "Paste a list" door (`?import=1`) is the Import door D6 hands it to, so row 166 closed without a home link. "Follow the build on GitHub" went; the footer's existing GitHub link is D6's "moves to the footer".
- **Measured** (Brave through the DevTools MCP; dev, both themes, signed out; the pages wrote nothing). Page heights: 1440 3,554 px (2,717 before), 768 4,644 (2,906), 390 5,720 (5,029), 360 5,679 (4,988); no horizontal overflow from 360 to 1440 (320 px scrolls 20 px sideways from the header, LATER row 250); 27 `<img>` in the server HTML (24 before), the fan's three eager with React's three high-priority preloads, the rest lazy; contrast 0 failures in both themes at 1440 (143 elements) and 390 (140), the primary's boundary 4.34 on dark and 8.12 on light. ### U6 decisions and deviations from `WAVE5.md` D7 (2026-10-15, `58cc253`)

D7's share B shipped in one session as one code commit. No migration, no route (the `pnpm build` route table byte-identical; `/d/[publicId]` stays `ƒ` and answers `private, no-cache` on prod), no dependency. What the package decided:

- **The header (B).** `SURFACE_BAND.deck` is `h-40 sm:h-40 md:h-70` (160 px below `md`, 280 from it; the `sm:` step overrides the hub default's `sm:h-44`). `DECK_HEADER` in `surface-header.tsx` holds the classes the share view and its loading shell share: from `md` a grid of the card column and the title column, the card column `md:-mt-28` so the card rises 112 px (40 % of the band) and `relative` so it paints over the band; below `md` the card sits above the title (the comp's phone form). The card is 200 px from `md` and 140 below it; the title column holds the H1, the two meta lines, the status lines, "At the table" and the action rows; the description follows under the grid at reading width. Measured on dev at 1440 / 1024 / 768: the overlap 112 px (40 %), the card 16 px in from the band's edge; the band's credit chip and the card's artist and © strip 0 of 84 sampled points covered (`elementFromPoint`) at 1440, 768, 390 and 360.
- **The gutters are 16 px, not the comp's 24 (a deviation, for LATER row 177).** With the comp's 24 px the title column at 768 is 488 px; the owner's row (Log a game, Open in editor, Copy, Share…, Buy this deck) needs 491.7 and wrapped, while the visitor's needs 456.9. A guest owner's row swaps in after hydration (the claim token is client-only), so at 768 the page dropped 36 px (0.0113; 52 px with touch, an iPad in portrait). With 16 px the column is 504 px and both rows hold one line: measured with a seeded claim token (no row minted), no shift at 360, 390, 768 (mouse and touch) or 1024. The swap still moves the page where one row wraps and the other doesn't: a single column 489 to 523 px wide (as before U6) and a partner deck's header from 853 to 887 px (row 177 annotated).
- **The header card.** `CardImage` `normal` at the 488 × 680 aspect, `priority` (eager, `fetchpriority="high"`, React's image preload, no fade), U8's `HERO_CARD_SHADOW`, CardImage's `frame` (the hover ring and the 2 px lift, `motion-safe:`, 120 ms on settle-120; under reduced motion no lift, the ring still answers hover). It is a plain `<button>` named "Show {name}" that opens the card's page, with no `data-slot`, because the visitor-row pin collects every `data-slot=button` inside the banner. The leader zone's validation ring and its sr-only "Has a problem" / "Warning" (R6) moved onto it through `aria-describedby`; the persistent accent ring did not (the comp's B carries the tinted shadow instead). Partners stand side by side at 160 px from `md` (120 below); a deck with no leader yet has no card column and the title column alone.
- **The meta lines (F's default words).** Line 1 is "{leader} · {format}": the leaders' names in the foreground, partners joined with " & " in `leaderIds` order, a One Piece card's printed id after its name in muted ink as the rows show it ("Enel OP15-058 · Standard", so the two Enel leaders still differ in text, C13), the format; the stat line (P/T, Power and Life) is left to the card itself, so `leaderLine` left `leader-caption.ts` with its last caller. Line 2 is "{N} cards · Updated {date} by {author}": N stays "100 / 100" (the count against the format's maximum, the editor's and the precon line's), the date stays UTC with the year (the hydration pin), "by {author}" only with a public username. Both lines are `.type-meta` (12 / 16) muted, one middle dot each. A deck without a leader reads the format alone on line 1. A precon keeps its chrome: line 1 the same, then the badge with the count, the product line and the byline, their words unchanged (the product line's two dots are U9's), all on `.type-meta`.
- **The status lines take the editor's slot forms, and the switch goes.** Every caller of `ValidationPanel` and `BracketLine` now wants the status slot (the editor since U3a, the share page now), so the `slot` prop and the non-slot markup are gone and the editor drops two props with nothing re-rendered. On the share page the problem toggle sits on the 20 px line with its dot in the 16 px gutter, and the bracket line brings the number forward with a quiet "Why?" (every text pin unchanged). **The bracket line stays 12 / 18** (`.type-meta-multiline`, U8's ladder), not D7's 13 px: the comp's hand-rolled 13 / 20 predates the ladder, and a size utility beside a ladder class is the thing U8 removed.
- **The heading tree and the blocks.** h1, then h2s only, then h3s: "At the table" (a serif h2 on `.type-small`, the ladder's small step, since `.type-section` would outweigh its box; no longer the uppercase label), then Cards, Notes, Sample hand and Analytics on `.type-section`, each naming its region (`aria-labelledby`), then the cost groups' h3s and the analytics blocks' titles. The leader zone's block and its h3 are gone (the card leads), which fixed Lighthouse's `heading-order` on `/d/jhr5ax43ewx7` (98 → 100; the Magic page was 100 because "At the table" stood between the h1 and the h3s). "Cards" is a visible h2 over the controls row (D7's tree; the comp drew the row above the h2), the row the editor's: "View" an `aria-label`, Group and Sort labelled. The cost groups' labels are the page's only uppercase text (11 uppercase labels before on the Magic page: At the table, Commander and six groups, Notes, the Sample hand trigger, Analytics).
- **The rows are the editor's read-only rows.** `ruled` is gone from `DeckTextView`, `DeckGridView`, `LeaderZone` and `SampleHand` (with `RULED_GROUP_HEADER_CLASS`): the groups are the label-and-count h3s on space, the count 14 px medium tabular, the pips 11.2 px (`--pip-size: 0.8em`, row 227's share half), read-only rows 24 px. `LeaderZone` keeps its read-only shape with no caller (LATER row 252).
- **Notes, Sample hand, Analytics.** Notes at `max-w-reading` (672 px, was 992 at 1440). `SampleHand`'s `titled` is the share page's block: a serif h2 over its region, "Draw sample hand" the dealer, no collapsible, nothing at all for an empty library; the editor keeps its collapsible beside `AnalyticsPanel`'s (row 221 annotated). LATER row 143 fired with the sample hand's change and is fixed: `toSmallImage` derives the mirror's small WebP for a One Piece card, so a deal is seven ~10 KB WebPs instead of ~174 KB PNGs.
- **Analytics: a share variant, not a global change.** `AnalyticsBlocks` gains `variant="share"` for the share page alone; the editor's panel and phone tab and the hub's staples curve keep the panel look (LATER row 253). Histograms are 10 px bars in `--chart-1` (the brand's chart color in both themes, the comp's) over no baseline in a `max-w-md` row, a target a thin dashed outline behind its bar, the value labels and the grow-in unchanged. Each breakdown is one stacked bar with a legend: both of Magic's ("Card types" and "Colors"; D7 named the first, and the renderer stays one form per block kind, game-ignorant), segments in proportion 1 px apart, a slice's own `colorVar` or the chart colors largest first and, past the fifth, muted ink at 40 % mixed in oklab (an oklch mix with transparent casts hue 0, U3b's finding), each segment and swatch with a faint inset edge (`ring-foreground/15`) so a pale slice (White on ivory) keeps its box; the bar is `aria-hidden` and the legend list says every count. The stat tiles are unchanged (D7 names none; the comp's serif number tiles were not taken).
- **The loading shell** draws the B header on `DECK_HEADER`: the 160 / 280 px band, one leader's box rising into it, the title, the two 16 px meta lines, the status line and the button row. A partner deck or a deck without a leader replaces the shell's boxes rather than moving them (the fallback is swapped out, and its `min-h-dvh` keeps the footer below the fold).

### U7b decisions and deviations from `WAVE5.md` D8 (2026-10-16, `8b8ddc0`)

D8's two remaining paragraphs (the hubs; the long lists on phones) shipped in one session as one code commit, no U7c split. No migration, no route (the `pnpm build` route table byte-identical; `/c/`, `/l/` and `/cards/[id]` stay `●`, `/sets` and `/precons` `○`), no dependency. What the package decided:

- **The one-explainer rule, against the pins.** D8 says the explainer renders once, under the first data section, and the other h2s stand alone; two of the lines are smoke-pinned (hubs-smoke reads "ranked by lists played", combos-smoke "most popular of N combos") and D8 itself keeps the Meta Lens wording byte-identical. The rule as shipped: every section opens with its serif h2 standing alone; the one explainer is the first data section's (on `/c/` the staples' "The 100 cards that fit this color identity …", on `/l/` "Find cards for this deck"'s one line); a line that carries a count, a floor or a source (the Meta Lens, the combos, the finishes) keeps every word and moves under its block as a source line, the convention of a chart's source note; a line that restated its heading is deleted, not rewritten (U9 owns copy): the template's "A starting template, not a rule …", the precons' "Official preconstructed decks with …", the decks' "Public Deckwarden decks running …", and on `/l/` "Same character, different card — each is its own archetype." The hub's heading tree is unchanged (h1, the seven h2s, the curve's h3 inside the Staples section) and Lighthouse a11y stays 100. `/l/` takes the same rule although D8 names `/c/` alone: the two hubs share one opening shape, so its one explainer moved from `text-sm` (14 / 20) to the ladder's meta step.
- **The explainer's step is `.type-meta-multiline` (12 / 18), not `.type-meta` (12 / 16).** Every kept line wraps on a phone (the staples' explainer two lines at 390, the Meta Lens line four, the finishes line two), and D3 gives the multi-line form 18 px of leading; the words are unchanged. The lines were `text-xs` (12 / 16) before.
- **The staples fold says the tier's count.** `StaplesTable` renders every row of the tier and hides the ones past 25 (`<tr hidden>`), so the ISR HTML carries all 100 (75 hidden) and nothing moves at hydration: the server render and the first client render share one state (the All tier, folded). The button is D8's "Show all 100" on the All tier and the tier's own count under a budget pill ("Show all 60" under ≤ $5 when 60 fit), nothing at 25 rows or fewer; the fold is one-way and outlives a tier change (a reader who asked for everything keeps everything); the rank column keeps counting the tier. No motion: the rows appear (D0 bars entry fades on page-like surfaces). Measured at 390: 25 → 100 rows on one click, the h2 unmoved, no layout-shift entry, the button 100.9 × 44 on touch (28 on a fine pointer).
- **The printings fold is the one existing button (a deviation from the prompt's two-button plan).** The prompt expected a 24-row button named apart from the tail fetch's pinned "Show all {total} printings", because `printings-gallery.test.tsx` asserts no button named "Show all" once the tail lands. Shipped instead: the 24-row fold and the tail share the one button. "Show all {total} printings" opens the hidden inline rows and, past the inline 100, fetches the tail in the same click, so one click shows everything the card has and the reader meets one idea; while the tail loads the 100 inline rows are already visible and the button says "Loading…" with `aria-busy`. The fold state is derived, never stored in an effect: `open = expanded || a filter is typed || the pinned row sits past the fold`, so a set filter shows its matches whole and a `?printing=` deep link to the 50th row opens the fold rather than hiding "Shown". After a capped tail the button stays while rows are folded (a crafted deep link on a card past the 250 cap, LATER row 88) and the cap note follows once they open. Every existing test held; the card page's inline 100 rows carry 76 hidden in the HTML.
- **The long lists collapse with CSS below `md` only.** D8's "`hidden` like `/sets`' other products" was read as "present in the HTML", not as the attribute: the `hidden` flags on `/sets`' `li` and sections stay the filter's alone (the page test's regex, the sets smoke's hidden-row count of the other products and `shownNames` all read them), and a collapsed year is `max-md:hidden` on the group's list with a "Show {year}" button and its count ("8 sets", "5 precons") beside the year's h2 below `md`; from `md` every group shows as before (so the desktop reader of "every set on one page" still scans the whole list), and every tile and link is in the server HTML at every width. The first two groups with a row to show are open (`/sets` counts the years the filter leaves, so with "Main sets only" on the two open years are the two newest with a main set), the rest collapse until asked; any filter opens every group (narrowing the list is the ask); Oldest keeps the two groups at the top of the list open (the ones a reader sorted to see first); Name sort is one flat group with nothing to fold. The collapsed sections sit 12 px apart below `md` (24 open): at 24 the `/sets` target was missed by 10 px (5,010 at 390), at 12 it is 4,626. Measured at 390: `/precons` 33,267 → 4,047 px (D8: under 6,000), `/sets` 18,066 → 4,626 (under 5,000), 4,047 and 4,746 at 360; the "Show {year}" row 49 px (the 44 px button beside the 14 px h2 over the hairline), the button 91.2 × 44; opening 2016 on `/precons` reveals its five tiles (34 → 39 visible) and on `/sets` its eight main sets (30 → 38 links), the other-product rows still hidden by the filter; "Main sets only" off adds 1996 as a collapsed year and the fold keeps counting; typing opens everything. LATER row 135's trigger fired: a no-JS phone reader sees the two newest years and buttons that do nothing (annotated, still deferred).
- **The year strip is native anchors.** A `<nav aria-label="Jump to year">` under the filters holds one `<a href="#precons-2016">` / `#sets-2016` chip per group with a row to show (the ids are new; no existing id moved), the color chips' class (24 px on a fine pointer, 44 on touch); a rail that scrolls sideways below `md` (`/sets`' 34 chips are 2,235 px wide at 390; LATER row 257) and wraps from it (`/precons` 24 px tall at 1440, `/sets` two rows, 54 px). A hash is a scroll position, not filter state: the islands never read it, the canonical is untouched, and the never-touches-the-URL tests gain a "Show {year}" click (a button). The sets island test's link census ("every `<a>` is a set link") is scoped to `a[href^="/cards?set="]` with a second assertion that every other anchor is a `#sets-` jump: a pin moved, said here. A jump to a year near the page's end scrolls as far as the page allows (the section can't reach the viewport top; browser behaviour, not a bug); `/sets`' 2016 lands exactly at the top.
- **`DeckTile` gains `compact`, for `/precons` alone.** Below `md` the tile is a row: the card at the search rows' 26 px (`w-[1.625rem]`, 36.3 px tall at the card's ratio), `p-2`, the game chip and the format `display: none` (one game and one format on the page that asks for it), so the meta reads "Precon · Released Oct 2026 · ≈ $41" on one line under the name and a precon is a 60 px row (34 rows and the collapsed years instead of 34 tiles of 164 px). **The card is `self-start`**: beside the 40 px text column the flex row stretched the 26 px card to 40 px and `object-cover` cropped its artist line (measured 26 × 40 before, 26 × 36.3 after); D0 never crops the artist and © line. From `md` the tile is everywhere's.
- **`/sets`' phone row stays two lines (a deviation from D8's "compact one-line list below `md`").** The row below `md` is the name and the count over code · place · date (64 px on touch); a one-line form would drop the code and the full date from the phone row or truncate names ("Teenage Mutant Ninja Turtles Source Material"), and the 390 target was met without it (LATER row 256).
- **`role="group"`** on `/precons`' game pills (LATER row 136, closed) and on its color identity filter, so both names reach assistive tech as `/sets`' pills already did. Lighthouse a11y was 100 before and after (an unnamed group is not a Lighthouse failure).
- **LATER row 107 fired and folded**: both hubs' finishes shelves read `src/lib/tournaments/format.ts`'s `ordinal` and `eventDateLabel` (byte-identical to the local copies, which are gone).
- **Not taken, by the owner's call or by measurement**: the hub's art-crop veil at 85 % (d5; the owner said Defer at pre-flight, LATER row 209); the share analytics variant on the hub's staples curve (the panel stays; the curve's 12 px Geist h3 sits unchanged inside the Staples section at 1440 and 390; LATER row 253); the hubs' pips (row 227: measured, the `/c/` title's pip span is the h1's sibling and inherits 16 px, so its pips are 11.52 px, the card page's 12.96, the precon tiles' 10.08; D8 names no pip change).
- **Measured on dev** (Brave through the DevTools MCP, signed out, both themes, 1440 / 768 / 390 / 360; `/c/atraxa-praetors-voice`, `/l/arlong-op03-022`, `/cards/459562ce-…` (Sol Ring, 138 printings), `/precons`, `/sets`).
